- 2026-10-13: Hit / kill marker, Call of Duty style (KAN-13, branch `feat/kan-13-hit-marker`, from KAN-18 head).
  - `UI/Hud/HitMarkerHud` (on HUD object): 4 diagonal ticks around a gap, at the hit enemy's `AimPoint` (follows the body; screen centre if off screen). Hit = white, pop 1.2 -> 1, 0.2 s. Kill = bigger red ticks, pop 1.45 -> 1, 0.38 s. Hold 35 % of life, then ease-out fade.
  - Listens to `Enemy.Damaged` (already carries the kill flag: no new events needed). Fixed pool of 6 built in OnEnable; same enemy hit again restarts its marker (kill upgrades it, hit never downgrades a kill); pool full = reuse oldest. No allocations per hit. Layer inserted under the joystick, picking ignored.
  - Looks: `hit-marker*` in GameHud.uss (dark 1.5 px border keeps white readable on pale stone).
  - Sounds: `SfxBuilder` adds `sfx_hitmarker_tick_01..02` (bright 3.4 kHz tick, 70 ms) + `sfx_killmarker_01` (thunk + two-note ping, 220 ms). `GameAudio` plays them on `Enemy.Damaged` (hit ticks rate limited 40 ms; kill always). SFX volume from GameSettings.
  - Tests: `Tests/PlayMode/HitMarkerTests` (7): hit marker, kill marker, repeat hits reuse 1 marker, hit then kill upgrades, pool caps at capacity, expiry (kill outlives hit), no GC alloc. All 13 PlayMode tests pass.
  - DevScreenshot `-ss-hit-marker <hit|kill>` pops a marker on the nearest enemy before capture. Screenshots `Docs/screenshots/game-hit-marker.png`, `game-kill-marker.png`.

- 2026-10-13: Light haptic on enemy kill (KAN-12, branch `feat/kan-12-kill-haptics`, from KAN-13 head). Scheduled-task run.
  - `Core/Haptics`: static `Light()` (only entry point for game code) + `Warmup()`. Checks `GameSettings.Vibration`, then rate limit `MinInterval` 50 ms unscaled (leading edge: group kill in one frame = 1 tap). `PulseCount` for tests. Backend per platform in the same file (`#if`):
    - iOS: native `Plugins/iOS/SSHaptics.mm` (`UIImpactFeedbackGenerator` light, one static generator, `prepare` after each tap keeps Taptic warm). `DllImport("__Internal")`.
    - Android: Vibrator via `AndroidApplication.currentContext` (API 31+ `VibratorManager.getDefaultVibrator`). Effect built once: amplitude control -> `createOneShot(14 ms, 64/255)`; else API 29+ `EFFECT_TICK`; else one-shot default amplitude. `hasVibrator` false -> silent. Pulse = raw `AndroidJNI.CallVoidMethod` with cached method ID + `jvalue[]` (no managed alloc). Java exception -> one warning, then off.
    - Editor / desktop: no-op.
  - Android permission: `Editor/Platform/AndroidVibratePermission` (`IPostGenerateGradleAndroidProject`, `#if UNITY_ANDROID`) adds `android.permission.VIBRATE` to unityLibrary manifest. Unity only adds it itself when `Handheld.Vibrate` is used.
  - `Combat/KillHaptics` on the `Game` object (GameSceneBuilder): `Enemy.Damaged` killed -> `Haptics.Light()`. Warmup in Awake (no JNI hitch on first kill).
  - Main menu: turning the Vibration toggle on plays one preview tap.
  - `DevBuildTools.BuildAndroidApk` (`-buildTarget Android ... -ss-out <file.apk>`, `-ss-dev` optional).
  - Tests: `Tests/PlayMode/HapticsTests` (7): kill = 1 pulse, hit = 0, 5 kills same frame = 1, rate limit window, vibration off = 0, editor backend no throw, kill no GC alloc. All 20 PlayMode tests pass.
  - Verified: scene rebuild 0 errors; Android APK build OK (`aapt2 dump permissions` shows VIBRATE); iOS Xcode build + xcodebuild device build OK (`SSHaptics.mm` compiled + linked).
  - Not verified: real tap feel on iPhone / Android. iPhone install failed this run: phone was locked (`IXRemoteErrorDomain code 6`). No Android phone connected. Redeploy and feel test by hand.

- 2026-10-13: Wind trail behind the running Knight (KAN-14, branch `feat/kan-14-wind-trail`, from KAN-12 head). Scheduled-task run.
  - `FX/WindTrail` (on player): one world-space particle system, emission module off. Script emits each particle with `EmitParams` (own spawn point + velocity from the current planar move direction), so a quick turn bends the trail. Streaks: 26/s, 0.06-0.1 m wide, 0.45-1.7 m high around the body, drift back 2.2-3.4 m/s, pale alpha 0.5. Specks: 9/s at the feet, dust or leaf tint (30 %), small lift.
  - Intensity: 0 below 2.2 m/s (standing / shooting / slow drift), smoothstep to 1 at 5 m/s. Ramp up 7/s, down 3.5/s (~0.3 s tail-off) + live particles fade out: no hard cut. Max 3 emits per frame, debt capped (no burst after a hitch). `Tick(velocity, dt)` public for tests.
  - `Editor/Effects/WindTrailBuilder` (called by GameSceneBuilder): child "Wind Trail" under the player, maxParticles 30, stretched billboards (lengthScale 1.6, velocityScale 0.22), drag 2.5, alpha fade in/out, size shrink. Material `Art/Materials/M_FX_Wind.mat` (URP Particles/Unlit, alpha blend, shared soft dot). No lights, no shadows, no probes, no motion vectors.
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/WindTrailTests` (9): still = 0, slow drift = 0, rate grows with speed, particles drift against move dir and spawn behind, quick turn, stop tails off then 0, 30 budget + hitch, intensity curve, no GC alloc. All 29 PlayMode tests pass.
  - DevScreenshot `-ss-release <s>`: lets go of the `-ss-move` stick that long before capture. Screenshots `Docs/screenshots/game-wind-{run,turn,stop,idle}.png`.
  - Verified: scene rebuild 0 errors, mac dev build OK, screenshots OK. Not deployed to phone this run; frame cost on device not measured (30 particles, 1 draw call).

- 2026-10-13: Floating directional joystick with fading-direction look (KAN-16, branch `feat/kan-16-floating-joystick`, from KAN-14 head). Scheduled-task run.
  - `Controls/FloatingJoystick` rewritten: output is `Move` (was `Value`; `PlayerMover` + HUD hint updated). One finger owns the stick by touch id: a 2nd finger never moves, steals or releases it; when the owner lifts, a still-held 2nd finger does not take over (needs a new touch). Mouse = finger -1 (editor), WASD fallback. Radius 120 ref px (1080 wide HUD), dead zone 0.1, centre follows finger past radius. `IgnoreStartAt` predicate (screen pos -> skip) set by the view: presses on UI Toolkit `Button`s never start the stick. Top 18 % strip still ignored. `Tick()` public (Update calls it).
  - `UI/Hud/JoystickView` (new, on HUD object, wired by GameSceneBuilder): ring + 4 chevrons (USS only: square with 2 borders turned 45 deg, side containers rotated 0/90/180/270) + small knob. Idle: bottom centre, `idleBottomMargin` 150 panel px above `Screen.safeArea` bottom (home indicator), all chevrons alpha 0.5, no knob. Touch: jumps to the finger. Drag: chevron alpha = `SideAlpha` = lerp(faint 0.2, clamp01(dot(dir, side)), drag length) (diagonal = 0.71 on both sides), smoothed 0.08 s. Release: glides back to idle (0.12 s), knob fades. Moves with `style.translate`, writes a style only when the value changed. Drawing removed from `GameHudController` (pause + hint only). Hint moved up (margin-bottom 480) above the idle stick. Chevrons sit just outside the ring so the knob never hides the active one.

Shipped table (200 runs): crowd power 2.0x at L5, 5.4x at L10, 16.5x at L20, but single-target only 2.1x at L20 (boss fights; see KAN-53).

- 2026-10-15: Multi-arrow rework: Double Shot + Spread Shot (KAN-83, also does KAN-36, branch `feat/kan-83-double-spread-shot`, from main a09acba). Scheduled-task run. Finished leftover WIP of an earlier run (worktree `kan-83-multi-arrow`, uncommitted, idle 12 h; ported, not touched).
  - `Combat/VolleyBuilder`: one volley = list of `VolleyArrow` (yaw, delay, side offset, damage share, chain step, lead) from a `VolleySpec` (stats). Fills a caller array, no alloc. Spread Shot fan: main bolt straight, side bolts +15, -15, +30, -30, +45, -45 deg. Double Shot chain: whole fan again every 0.09 s, side step 0.18 m (0, +, -, ++). Both together: every fan bolt repeats. Hard cap 12 bolts per volley (later chain steps lose outer bolts first).
  - `AutoShooter`: builds the volley through VolleyBuilder (no skill code in the weapon); follow-ups wait in a fixed 48-slot queue (each keeps its own volley's shot), active bolt budget 72 (over it: bolt skipped), `FollowUpFired` per chain step (CrossbowRecoil 50 % kick, GameAudio quiet pitched shot layer 35 %, CombatFx puff). `Fired` once per volley = one reload ring sweep; attack speed applies to the volley. `SetBoltLimits`, `DescribeVolley`, `CancelPending` (OnDisable).
  - Stats: `StatId.DoubleShot` (extra chain bolts), `SpreadDamage` (side bolt share, shooter base 0.5), `ChainDamage` (follow-up share, base 1). `Multishot` now = Spread Shot side bolts.
  - Skills: `Skill_Multishot.asset` reworked in place (same guid + id `multishot`: saves / debug configs load) to Spread Shot, Rare, 3 levels: 3 / 5 / 7 bolts, side bolts 50 / 65 / 80 %. New `Skill_DoubleShot.asset` (id `double_shot`, Epic, 3 levels: 2 / 3 / 4 bolts, L3 follow-ups 90 %), icon `T_Skill_DoubleShot.png`, level 2 tier unlock, level 3 guarantee (KAN-36 early offer). SkillsBuilder `ReworkFrom` migration (rewrites once while the old name is on the asset) + `LateGuarantees`.
  - Rare stacking: `SkillDefinition.repeatOfferWeights` (Double Shot 0.25 at owned L1, 0.06 at L2) scale the level-up weight; a damped skill is never forced back by its guarantee or the pity rule. `upgradeOfferWeight` (Double Shot 0.35) scales its chest card. `SkillStackOdds.Simulate` (1000 runs, levels 2-20, no chest upgrades): 2+ stacks 11.6 % (random card) / 51 % (always taken), 3 stacks 0.4 % / 4.4 %.
  - Decision (ticket asked): Double Shot repeats every Spread Shot bolt, total capped at 12 per volley. Spread Shot side share starts at 50 % (ticket proposed 60 %): two side bolts and no main-bolt penalty; keeps the Warden 10-pick fight at 66 s (target 60-90).
  - Power curve: `PowerSnapshot` counts chain (single target) and side bolts at their share (crowd), with the cap. Balance test reads the side share.
  - Debug menus: "Volley: Double Shot Level 0..3", "Volley: Spread Shot Level 0..3" (Play mode, set level + print), "Volley: Print", "Volley: Print Double Shot Stack Odds"; window button "Print volley". DevScreenshot `-ss-volley` (volley list + stack odds to the log).
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/MultiArrowTests` (25). Updated: SkillLevelTableTests (14 skills, Spread Shot 3 levels), SkillRarityTests, BossIntroTests balance, SkillDebugTelemetryTests crowd DPS. Full suite: 424 pass, 2 skipped (soak), 0 fail.
  - Shots (540x1170, `-ss-scene Game -ss-immortal`): `Docs/screenshots/game-kan83-spread-double-l1.png` (`-ss-skills multishot:1,double_shot:1 -ss-horde 8 -ss-delay 4.3`: fan + follow-ups), `game-kan83-volley-horde60.png` (`-ss-skills multishot:3,double_shot:3 -ss-horde 60 -ss-hold 60 -ss-delay 3.6`). Mac: 55 enemies + 12-bolt volleys 17.1 ms frame (vsync), 207 draw calls, ~1M tris (horde cost, not bolts).

- 2026-10-15: Bug fix, Mac dev player ignored `-ss-quality Mobile` (KAN-52, branch `feat/kan-52-mac-mobile-quality`, fix written 2026-10-14 from main a09acba, cherry-picked onto main 667df8d). Scheduled-task run. Finished leftover work of an earlier run (worktree `kan-52-mac-mobile-quality`, 3 commits never merged, idle 11 h; ported, not touched).
  - Cause: the Mobile quality level excludes Standalone (`ProjectSettings/QualitySettings.asset`), so the OSX player only had PC; `-ss-quality Mobile` logged `Unknown quality level 'Mobile'` and every Mac screenshot / draw-call log since KAN-25 came from PC_RPAsset, not the phone level.
  - Fix (`Editor/DevBuildTools.cs`): `BuildMacDev` runs the build inside `WithQualityLevelOnStandalone("Mobile", ...)`: removes the Standalone exclusion (SerializedObject), builds, puts it back at the same index, clears the dirty flag (else the editor re-serializes the file on exit), and writes the original bytes back if they changed. Also on a failed build (`finally`). Player still starts on PC (Standalone default); `-ss-quality Mobile` switches to Mobile_RPAsset. iOS / Android builds and the editor are unchanged. `IsQualityLevelOnStandalone(name)` for checks.
  - `Scripts/Dev/DevScreenshot.cs`: logs `[DevScreenshot] quality=<level> pipeline=<URP asset>` after the override and appends it to the `Stats` line; an unknown level lists the levels the player has.
  - Tests: first EditMode assembly `Tests/EditMode` (`SurvivorSouls.Tests.EditMode`, refs SurvivorSouls.Editor). `DevBuildToolsTests` (3): Mobile on Standalone only inside the build action, exclusion + file bytes restored, not dirty after; a throwing build still restores; unknown level runs the action unchanged. Run: `-runTests -testPlatform EditMode`. EditMode 3 pass; PlayMode 424 pass, 2 skipped (soak), 0 fail.
  - Shots (540x1170, `-ss-scene Game -ss-delay 5 -ss-horde 40 -ss-immortal`): `-ss-quality Mobile` -> `Docs/screenshots/devbuild-quality-mobile.png` (`quality=Mobile pipeline=Mobile_RPAsset`, 112 draw calls, 32 SetPass, 406k tris), `-ss-quality PC` -> `devbuild-quality-pc.png` (`quality=PC pipeline=PC_RPAsset`, 160 draw calls, 41 SetPass, 806k tris). Older STATE numbers marked "Mobile quality" were PC numbers.
  - Verified: compile 0 errors, Game scene rebuild exit 0 (fileID churn only, not committed), mac dev build + 2 screenshot runs without exceptions, QualitySettings.asset unchanged after the build, iOS export + xcodebuild + install + launch on iPhone 14 Pro Max OK.

Shader variants of both URP assets go into the Mac dev player (bigger build, dev only). Old worktree `kan-52-mac-mobile-quality` holds the same work uncommitted (STATE + screenshots) and scene churn; safe to remove.

- 2026-10-15: Souls wallet, first slice of the KAN-89 economy (KAN-96, branch `feat/kan-96-soul-wallet`, from main 9082923). Scheduled-task run. KAN-89 split into KAN-96 (this), KAN-97 gems, KAN-98 soul skill tree, KAN-99 store + mock IAP.

Saved on every change. A balance that fails its checksum (hand edit) or is negative resets to 0, logs a warning, `TamperDetected` true. Casual edits only; real protection needs a server.

Hooks: `PlayerHealth.OnDeath`, `ChamberTransition.RealmCleared` (refs `health` / `transition` on RunCounters, wired by GameSceneBuilder), `GameHudController.LeaveToMenu` (pause / Esc). Death + victory screens: `+1,240 BANKED · 12,480 SOULS HELD` (`death-wallet` / `victory-wallet`, class `death__wallet`). Main menu: wallet pill top right (`lbl-wallet-souls`, placeholder until the KAN-56 currency bar).

PlayMode `SoulBankTests` (5) + `WalletPrefsGuard` SetUpFixture (saves / restores the editor wallet around the whole PlayMode suite, because Game scene tests now bank). EditMode 11 pass; PlayMode 429 pass, 2 skipped.

Game 42 draw calls Mobile.

- 2026-10-15: Zombie power scaling to the Knight's power, danger colour tiers, 2 minute Chambers (KAN-92, branch `feat/kan-92-horde-power-scaling`, from main 9082923). Scheduled-task run.
  - `Scripts/Gameplay/Combat/HordeScaling.cs` (asset `Data/Combat/HordeScaling_Realm1.asset`, builder never overwrites): power index P = 1-target DPS ratio ^ 0.65 x crowd DPS ratio ^ 0.35 over the bare Knight (KAN-75 `PowerSnapshot` formulas: damage, fire rate, crit, Spread / Double Shot, pierce, ricochet, burn). Zombie health x P ^ 0.6 x (1 + 0.3 x Chamber clock progress), cap x6, on top of the KAN-46 Chamber multiplier. Exponent < 1: the Knight nets P ^ 0.4, so every skill still pays off. `Table(curve)` = balance table.
  - Danger tiers: total health multiplier -> tier value between thresholds x1 / 1.6 / 2.5 / 4 / 6.5 (calm, bile green, amber, crimson, violet), 3 colour steps per tier (13 steps). `HordeTierMaterials`: one shared copy of the body material per step and type (_BaseColor = type tint x step colour), so the SRP Batcher stays on; a calm tinted zombie has no property block. `Enemy.SetDangerStep`, respawn resets to the own material. Bosses: Chamber health only, own colour.
  - `Scripts/Gameplay/Chamber/HordeScaler.cs` on the "Chamber" object (`ChamberBuilder.CreateHordeScaler`): re-measures P on `PlayerStats.Changed` only, reads the clock, pushes `EnemySpawner.SetHordeScale` every frame (no alloc). Zombies on the field keep their spawn health. Boss Chambers: no clock ramp.

  - Debug: menu "Survivor Souls/Debug/Horde: Print Scaling Table" (Chamber 1 + Chamber 9); DevScreenshot `-ss-horde-power <P>` (override), `-ss-horde-tiers` (one Shambler per colour step, shooter off).
  - Balance (shipped skill table, 200 runs, random picks): level 10 P 3.7 -> zombie hp x2.2 (x2.8 at Chamber end), Knight edge 1-target 1.07x, crowd 3.9x; level 20 P 8.5 -> hp x3.6 (x4.7), edge 1-target 1.06x, crowd 10.4x. Time to kill a 100 HP Shambler single-target stays ~0.95 s (1.23 s at Chamber end).
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/HordeScalingTests` (16): power index (bare = 1, weighted geometric mean, never below 1), health power / clock / cap, skills stay strong, tier values / steps / colours, shipped tiers, shared tier materials, calm tier zombie without property block + respawn reset, spawner scales regular spawns not bosses, old behaviour without scaling, no GC on tiered respawns and scaler refresh, live scaler follows stats + clock (boss Chamber: no ramp), shipped balance (1-target edge >= 1, crowd edge > 4 at level 20), Game scene wiring. `ChamberTests.ShippedRules_AreTwoMinutes`. Soak (`EnemySpawnTests.Soak`) now stops the Chamber clock: with 2 minute Chambers the portal opened mid-soak and a wandering Knight reloaded the scene (timeout, then later no-alloc tests failed on the leftover scene). After merging main (KAN-96): PlayMode 445 pass, 2 skipped (soak), 0 fail; EditMode 11 pass.

Mac Mobile: 114 draw calls with tiers and without (tier materials add none), 16.9 ms frame (vsync).

Per character: SK = Generic + Mesh LODs (4k -> 2.1k / 1.1k / ... tris), no FBX materials; A_ clips Generic, Walk / Shamble / Walking / Running / Sprint + action clips loop, Hit / Death once. Action clips > 3.2 s cut once to a 1.35 s strike window round the fastest hand / foot (Chop, Throw, Shoot, Leap, Grab, Summon, CastNova, CastRing); a range already set (by hand too) is kept. Textures max 1024, Normal as normal map. `M_<Id>.mat` Simple Lit (base + normal + emission map where `T_<Id>_Emission.jpg` exists). `AC_<Id>.controller`: Move (default), Attack (signature clip), Hit (plays in 0.7 s), Death (0.9 s; bosses 2 s) + one state per clip name. `Prefabs/Enemies/Enemy_<Id>.prefab`.
  - Rig fact: the A_ clip paths start at `Hips` (no rig node), the SK has `<Id>_Rig/Hips`. So the Animator sits on `<Id>_Rig` and the skinned mesh is moved under it (model instance unpacked; Animator culling needs the renderer below it). Clips are already in place (hips drift < 10 % of the height per loop, tested).
  - Sizes from the bind-pose mesh: heights = roster doc (Laborer 1.72 ... Colossus 5.0 m); bosses 2.29-2.86x the Knight. Hit radius = 70th pct of the chest band, at least 0.25 x height (old zombie parity, 0.43-0.46 m for the tiers). Contact / stop reach scale with height ^ 0.75 (tiers ~1.4 / 1.1 m). Stats = roster doc section 3 (code table, like CombatBuilder). Specials: 8 dps contact placeholder; bosses: 30 dps, knockback 0.1, no XP (BossData will own health).
  - Walk clip choice: the doc clip unless the type speed would play it outside 0.4-2.4x; then the locomotion clip closest to 1x. Militiaman (Shamble 0.37 m/s), Gravebound Knight and Bloater (Walk ~0.45 m/s) use `Walking` (Meshy human walk); Laborer / Watchman shamble at 2.3x / 2.1x; Ghoul sprints at 0.49x; Matriarch glides at 0.44x.
  - `Enemy`: Hit flinch on a direct, non-killing hit when the controller has "Hit" (not while attacking, never bosses, 0.4 s, 1.2 s cooldown; burn / burst ticks do not flinch). Kill with a "Death" state: plays it (state speed sets the time), no tip-over, sinks after `deathHold` (deeper for tall bodies). Old zombies unchanged (no such states). Dying bodies count against the cap: roster death = 1.6 s (old 1.05 s).

- 2026-10-15: Ranged zombies: Bone Archer, Pyre Thrower, Bile Spitter + the shared ranged framework (KAN-105 = KAN-103.2, branch `feat/kan-105-ranged-enemies`, from main dff38bc). Scheduled-task run.
  - Keep distance (`Enemy`): hold band `holdMin..holdMax` (0 / 0 = melee chaser). Farther than the band middle = walk in, closer = back off (0.8x speed), settle near the middle, then stand facing the Knight (`IsHolding`). `NeedsClearShot` = walk in. `ViewReach` caps the band to the screen edge in the enemy's direction (floor 3.5 m, width >= 1.5 m): the portrait view is ~11 m wide, so the doc bands (7-9 m) put shooters off screen sideways. `RangedAttack` measures it (one camera ground footprint per frame, margin 1.4 m).
  - `Scripts/Gameplay/Combat/Ranged/`: `EnemyProjectiles` (struct pool, 48; straight shots at chest height with a segment hit test, one hit per volley; lobbed parabola to a fixed ground point in a fixed time, impact only inside its circle, then a hazard; walls / pillars stop straight shots and low "blockable" lobs: no hazard then; one particle system per `ShotKind`, arrows = stretched billboard), `GroundHazards` (struct pool, 24; circle, life, dps, slow; the Knight takes the highest dps and slow of the circles it stands in, no stacking; max N per owner, oldest goes; one flat particle system per `HazardKind`; `PlayerMover.Slow`), `EnemyTelegraphs` (rents the boss `LineTelegraph` lanes (8 x 3 lanes) and `GroundTelegraph` circles (16); no free warning = no attack), `RangedAttack` (Idle -> WindUp (Halted: plays the Attack clip; aim lanes follow the Knight then lock for the last `lockShare`; lobbed circles appear at once at the lead-aimed points) -> release -> 0.35 s recover -> cooldown; pattern cycles, e.g. 1, 1, 3; kill / dead Knight cancels and hides the warnings; line-of-fire check every 0.25 s for straight shooters).
  - Numbers (`Editor/Enemies/RangedEnemyBuilder.Specs`, roster doc 3.6-3.8): Archer band 7-9, 0.8 s aim (last 35 % locked), arrow 14 m/s 14 m 10 % max HP, 1-1-3 (fan 30 deg), cooldown 2.2 s. - 2026-10-15: Boss attack pattern system + The Butcher of Ashgrave as the Realm 1 boss (KAN-108 = KAN-103.5, branch `feat/kan-108-boss-patterns`, from main 7081d74). Scheduled-task run. Split: Nova / Spiral / Rain / Lobbed / Summon / Teleport moved to KAN-116 (blocks KAN-109..111).
  - Data (`Scripts/Gameplay/Boss/BossAttack.cs`): one struct per attack: `type` (`BossFight.Move`: Slam, Fan, Charge, Lane, Spin), `phases` (Phase1 / Phase2 / Both), `priority`, `weight`, `minRange..maxRange`, `telegraph` (0.6 s floor), `recover`, `cooldown` (chase time after it), `damage` (share of max HP, capped by `maxHitShare`), numbers (`radius reach length width speed spread count duration interval pull stun`), `clip` (animator state). `BossData.attacks` + `roarClip` + `phaseRoar`; `BossData.Attacks` = the list, or (empty list) the Warden list built from its old slam / fan / charge fields, so the Warden plays as before. `BossData.CreateWithAttacks` for tests and the builder.
  - Pick (`BossFight.ChooseAttack`): entries on in this phase, Knight in range, scene has what it needs (lanes / bolts); highest `priority` band wins; inside the band score = weight x attacks since last use (ties: list order): equal weights take turns, higher weights come round more often. An attack that cannot start (charge with no room) drops out, the pick runs again; nothing fits = keep chasing.
  - New types: Lane (rectangle from the boss to the Knight, pillars / wall cut it via `BossFight.LaneLength`; `pull` > 0 = chain hook: the hit drags the Knight that far in at 18 m/s through `PlayerMover.Shove`, never closer than the boss body + 0.8 m). Spin (circle telegraph fills, then follows the boss for `duration` s while the boss walks to the Knight at `speed`; a hit at most every `interval` s; `GroundTelegraph.MoveTo`). Charge: `count` dashes in a row (each warns again, 0.25 s pause), a dash cut short by a pillar or the wall adds `stun` s to the recovery and ends the combo. Events `LaneWarned`, `LaneStruck`, `SpinHit`, `AttackStarted` (GameAudio + GameSceneController: lane = slam shake / sound, spin hit = light haptic).
  - Clips: `Enemy.PlayAction(state)` swaps the attack state (falls back to Attack when the controller lacks it) and plays it from the start; `ResetAction` after the attack. Intro plays `roarClip`; phase 2 (while chasing) halts the boss `phaseRoar` s with the roar.
  - Boss per boss Chamber: `BossFight.bosses` list; the n-th boss Chamber of the Realm (difficulty asset) gets entry n mod count, else `data`. Realm 1 list = [Butcher].
  - The Butcher (`Data/Boss/Boss_Realm1_Butcher.asset`, `BossBuilder.ButcherAttacks`, never overwritten): body `Enemy_AshgraveButcher` (4.2 m), 8000 HP, 2.4 s intro (Roar), phase 2 at 50 % with a 1.2 s roar. Phase 1: Cleaver Chop lane 6 x 1.6 m (0.9 s, 22 %, prio 1, weight 2, 0-5.5 m), Cleaver Spin r 3 m 2.5 s follow 1.6 m/s, 8 % per 0.6 s (prio 1, 0-4.5 m), Charge 11 m 15 m/s 20 % + 1 s stun (4.5-13 m). Phase 2: Chain Hook lane 10 x 1 m, 10 %, pull 4 m (5-10 m), Triple Charge 3 x 10 m 16 m/s 18 % each (weight 2). Balance: 12800 HP in Chamber 5, 10 picks -> 73 s, no picks 233 s (Warden 66 s).
  - Dev: `-ss-boss-attack slam|fan|charge|lane|spin|<index>` (Knight stands inside that attack's range). Menus "Survivor Souls/Debug/Boss: Slam / Lane (Chop / Hook) / Spin / Print Attacks".
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/BossPatternTests` (20): Warden list from old fields, priority band, turns + weights, phase gating, nothing in range, boss list per Chamber, lane warn / hit once capped / dodge / pillar cut, hook pull + stop outside the body, spin warn / follow / hit per interval / outside safe, triple charge (3 dashes, each warns, one pick), wall crash = stun + combo end, open ground no stun, phase 2 roar, kill hides the spin circle, dead Knight = no attacks, no GC; Game scene Chamber 5 = Butcher (Roar intro state, Chop state, lane inside the ring). `BossAttackTests` scene test now checks the Butcher's lane (it has no fan); `BossIntroTests` balance runs for Warden + Butcher.

With these three the Pyre Lord (KAN-109) has every type it needs (Fan + Nova + Slam + Spiral + Rain).

KAN-109 starts its list from these.
  - Nova (`Move.Nova`): wind-up = circle of `reach` m round the body (`GroundTelegraph`, 0.6 s floor), then `count` bolts evenly round the body (random turn per cast), `gaps` evenly spaced gaps of 2 bolts (`BossFight.RingDirections`, max 48 bolts). One ring hits once (`FireVolley`).
  - Spiral (`Move.Spiral`): same wind-up, then `Attack.Casting` (new state) for `duration` s: every `interval` s each of `count` arms fires a bolt, arms turn `spread` deg/s (the first arm starts at the Knight), the boss body turns with them. The whole spiral is one volley: it hits once (`BossProjectiles.NewVolley` + `Fire`). A full pool fires fewer bolts.
  - Rain (`Move.Rain`): the boss stands and casts (`Attack.Casting`); a circle every `interval` s, the first on the Knight, the rest within `reach` m of where the Knight stands then; each warns `telegraph` s (phase 2 factor, 0.6 s floor) and hits only inside its `radius`. Circles come from the shared `EnemyTelegraphs` pool (KAN-105; `BossFight.circles`, else `EnemyTelegraphs.Current`): no free circle = no rain; every circle goes back on strike, kill, dead Knight or disable. Max 16 per cast.

- 2026-10-15: Boss pattern types Lobbed, Summon, Teleport (KAN-119 = KAN-103.5c, branch `feat/kan-119-boss-lobbed-summon-teleport`, from main 980a0c2). Scheduled-task run. With these three, KAN-110 (Rotting Colossus: boulder + summon) and KAN-111 (Bone Matriarch: summon + teleport) are unblocked for their main types.
  - Data (`BossAttack`): new fields `obstacle` (rubble life s) and `summon` (roster id); factories `BossAttack.Lobbed / Summon / Teleport`; `Sample(Lobbed)` = Colossus Corpse Boulder (circle r 2.5 m, flight 1.1 s, 20 %, rubble r 0.9 m for 8 s, 3-14 m), `Sample(Summon)` = 4 Frenzied Ghouls on a 2.6 m ring (phase 2), `Sample(Teleport)` = Matriarch (7 m from the Knight, circle r 1.6 m, no damage, phase 2). KAN-110 / KAN-111 start their lists from these.
  - Lobbed (`Move.Lobbed`): wind-up shows the first landing circle on the Knight (shared `EnemyTelegraphs` pool); release throws, then `Attack.Casting`: one boulder per `interval` at where the Knight stands then, each with its own circle (warning = flight, 0.6 s floor); each boulder hits once inside its circle. No free circle = no throw. `Gameplay/Boss/BossBoulders` ("Boss/Boulders"): pool of 6 boulder bodies (one lumpy stone mesh `Art/Boss/Mesh_BossBoulder.asset`, Realm snow-cap props material, capsule collider each), parabola 4.5 m apex, tumbles. A landed boulder with `obstacle` > 0 becomes rubble: the Knight is pushed out first, then the collider blocks it, and `BossArena.Obstacles` (new static, set by BossBoulders) makes the arena walkability treat rubble as a pillar (enemies steer round, boss bolts stop, charges crash). Rubble sinks after its life (0.5 s, blocks nothing while sinking). Kill = all boulders, rubble and circles gone.
  - Summon (`Move.Summon`): the type resolves once per fight (`EnemySpawner.FindType("Enemy_" + id)`); wind-up = circles (r 0.9 m) on a ring of `reach` m round the boss (none on the Knight's line; a blocked spot moves in to 60 %), then `EnemySpawner.SpawnAt(type, p, onScreen: true)` (new arg: the off-screen audit skips summons). Never more than `count` alive: only the missing ones come; a dead summon is forgotten (`Enemy.Damaged`). Unknown type = never picked. Frenzied Ghoul joins the spawner extra types (Bone Archer already there).
  - Teleport (`Move.Teleport`): a walkable point `length` m from the Knight, on land, >= 3 m from the boss; in front of or behind the Knight first (<= 4 m sideways, `BossFight.TeleportMaxSide`: the portrait view is ~11 m wide), else any side; none = not picked. The main circle warns there; the boss shrinks to 5 % over the last 0.35 s of the wind-up, moves, grows back over 0.35 s (also restored on kill / Knight death). `damage` > 0 hits inside the circle on arrival.
  - Events `BoulderThrown`, `SummonWarned`, `Summoned`, `TeleportWarned`, `Teleported` (no sound / shake wired yet: KAN-120 / KAN-110 / KAN-111). `BossFight.Thrown`, `SummonsAlive`, `TeleportTarget`, `Boulders`. `EnemySpawner.SetExtraTypes` (tests / tools).

- 2026-10-15: Special melee zombies Plague Bloater, Frenzied Ghoul, Grave Brute (KAN-106 = KAN-103.3, branch `feat/kan-106-special-melee`, from main 69a7f63). Scheduled-task run.
  - Code: `Scripts/Gameplay/Combat/Melee/`: `SpecialAttack` (base: Idle -> WindUp -> Strike -> Recover, warnings rented from the shared `EnemyTelegraphs` pool, Knight from `EnemyProjectiles.Current`, events `Struck` / `HitKnight`, `Tick(dt)` public for tests, `ForceAttack`), `BloaterBurst`, `GhoulLeap`, `BruteSlam`. Contact damage stays on top.
  - Plague Bloater: within 2 m it stops, plays Burst, swells to 1.22x for 1 s under a green circle r 2.8 m (`GroundTelegraph.Tint`, back to red on Hide). Burst: Knight inside -25 % max HP; other zombies inside (not bosses) -50 % of their own max (`DamageKind.Burst`, chains); toxic cloud `HazardKind.Toxic` r 2.2 m 6 s 15 dps. Own burst = `Enemy.Expire()` (death without `Damaged`: no wisp, no kill). A Knight kill bursts the corpse 0.6 s after its warning first showed (a kill mid-swell keeps the shown time); no free circle = the corpse still bursts on zombies + cloud but never hits the Knight unwarned. Decision: 50 % = share of each zombie's own max HP.
  - Frenzied Ghoul: at 4-5 m (doc 4-6 m: from 6 m a 5 m leap lands short of a standing Knight) crouch 0.6 s (doc 0.5 s; 0.6 s warning rule) + red lane locked at the start, cut at pillars (`BossFight.FreeRun`, 0.25 m probes; < 3 m free = no leap). Leap 5 m in 0.35 s, arc 0.8 m, swept-path hit once (lane half width + 0.25 m) -10 %; lands, plays Hit as a 0.8 s stun, cooldown 3 s. First leap 0.5 s after spawn. - 2026-10-15: Bug fix, flaky soak test "pool covers the cap" (KAN-155, branch `feat/kan-155-deterministic-soak`, from main 10e783c). Scheduled-task run.
  - Cause: seeding was not the problem (SetUp already calls `Random.InitState(2029)`); frame times at timeScale 8 make every soak path different. Per-type pools are sized from the weighted share (x1.5, max 1.25x cap), so a luck streak on one type (plus its dying bodies) emptied that pool and `Take` instantiated mid-fight, while other types still had idle bodies.
  - Fix (`Combat/EnemySpawner`, `Combat/EnemyWaveTable`): `PickEnemy` takes an optional `Predicate<Enemy> stocked`. `SpawnPending` picks as before; when the picked type's pool is empty it re-picks with the same roll among phase types that have stock (cached delegate `_hasStock`, no alloc). New counter `Counters.PoolSwaps` (in the stats line / soak log). Play now instantiates only when every pool of the phase is empty. Gameplay mix unchanged except at a dry pool.
  - Second flake found and fixed: `ChamberSpawnTableTests.GameScene_ChamberGivesTheSpawnerItsChamber...` left the Game scene loaded; its Knight shot the next fixture's chaser (`EnemySpawnTests.Chaser_SteersAroundAWall` failed "got round the wall" in a filtered run). The test now unloads the scene like the soak.
  - Tests (`Tests/PlayMode/EnemySpawnTests`): stock-predicate pick cases; `Waves_DryTypePool_SwapsToStockedType_NoInstantiate` (type A drained by hand, waves fill the cap with B only, Created flat, PoolSwaps > 0). Soak assert tightened from `<= prewarm + 4` to `== prewarm` (every soak: created=82, poolSwaps 0-4).
  - Shot: `Docs/screenshots/game-kan155-natural-waves.png` (`-ss-scene Game -ss-quality Mobile -ss-immortal -ss-move 0.6,0.8 -ss-delay 30`: 18 natural spawns, poolSwaps=0, 64 draw calls).
  - Verified: compile 0 errors / 0 new warnings, Game scene rebuild exit 0 (fileID churn only, not committed), 3 full PlayMode runs in a row 627 pass / 2 skipped / 0 fail, EditMode 90 pass, mac dev build + screenshot run without exceptions, iOS dev export + xcodebuild + install + launch on iPhone 14 Pro Max OK.

- 2026-10-15: Scene rebuild no longer churns generated assets (KAN-157, branch `feat/kan-157-stable-rebuild`, from main 4e630ee). Scheduled-task run run-20261015-1809.
  - Before: every `GameSceneBuilder.BuildFromCommandLine` rewrote ~50 committed files with no real change: 15 `AC_*.controller` (CreateAnimatorControllerAtPath = new random fileIDs), 13 `Tile_*.prefab` (same-named children like "Block" get new fileIDs), `Game.unity` (~67k lines), 12 roster `M_<Enemy>.mat` + 9 `M_FX_*.mat`.
  - `Editor/StableAssetWriter.cs`: builders call `StableAssetWriter.Remember(path)` before they write (CombatBuilder / EnemyRosterBuilder / MeshyCharacterImporter controllers, WorldBuilder tiles, both scenes). `SettleAll()` at the end of `GameSceneBuilder.Build` / `MainMenuSceneBuilder.Build` saves, then writes the old bytes back for each file whose new text is equivalent: `UnityYamlCanon.AreEquivalent` hashes each YAML document with local `{fileID: N}` refs masked, refines with the hashes of referenced docs (Weisfeiler-Lehman), compares the multisets; external refs (with guid) stay literal; a prefab must keep its root GameObject fileID (other assets point at it). A real change keeps the new file and logs `[StableAssetWriter] Changed: <path>`. Log line per rebuild: `Kept 29 unchanged generated file(s)`.
  - Roster materials: `EnemyRosterBuilder.BuildMaterial` now runs the URP Simple Lit keyword pass (`BaseShaderGUI.SetMaterialKeywords(mat, SimpleLitGUI.SetMaterialKeywords)`, same as `ShaderGUI.ValidateMaterial`). Cause of the old flip: `EditorAssetUtil.SimpleLitMaterial` sets `_SpecularHighlights 1`, which URP reads as `NoSpecular`, but enables `_SPECULAR_COLOR`; the rebuild added the keyword, the next editor / EditMode test session (validation) removed it. Look unchanged (no specular, as on main). Editor asmdef now references `Unity.RenderPipelines.Universal.Editor`.
  - Committed once: 9 `M_FX_*.mat` (particle `disabledShaderPasses` + `_CameraFadeParams` as URP writes them) and `M_GraveyardLaborer.mat` (`_EMISSION` + realtime GI, as the KAN-109 builder writes it).
  - Tests `Tests/EditMode/StableAssetWriterTests` (8): identical, renamed ids + new document order = equivalent; changed value, swapped child order, new prefab root id, extra document = not; external refs literal; `SettleRemembered` restores churn only and keeps a real change (temp files under `Temp/`, no SaveAssets).
  - Verified: compile 0 errors / 0 new warnings; two Game scene rebuilds in a row + MainMenu rebuild leave `git status` clean; EditMode 112 pass, PlayMode 627 pass / 2 skipped / 0 fail, and the test runs after a rebuild leave `git status` clean; mac dev build + Game screenshot (`-ss-horde 40 -ss-quality Mobile`, 116 draw calls, no exceptions, no visible change: editor-only ticket) `Docs/screenshots/kan157-game-stable-rebuild.png`; iOS dev export + xcodebuild + install + launch on iPhone 14 Pro Max OK.
  - Limits: other builders that recreate assets (`SaveAsPrefabAsset` with unique child names) were already stable and are not wrapped; a new churning builder needs a `Remember` call. A symmetric hierarchy where two same-content children swap references could hash the same (WL limit); real edits change document text and are caught. `SimpleLitMaterial` still writes `_SpecularHighlights 1` + `_SPECULAR_COLOR` for world / zombie materials: KAN-158.
- 2026-10-15: Bug fix, boss legs kept running in place after a charge (KAN-162, branch `feat/kan-162-charge-stop-anim`, from main e5934a2). Scheduled-task run run-20261015-1955.
  - Cause: `BossFight.BeginRecover` only set `Attack.Recover`; the boss stayed `Halted`, so `Enemy` kept the attack state (`PlayAction(clip)`) until `EndAttack`. The Charge run looped through the recover, the 1 s crash stun and the redash pauses. Same for every other attack: the Chop / Spin / Slam / Cast clip looped while the boss stood still.
  - Fix: `Enemy.PlayPose(state)` plays a state only when the controller has it (no fallback to Attack). `BeginRecover(seconds, cooldown, RecoverPose)`: `Stand` (default) plays `Enemy.StandState` "Stand"; `Stunned` (dash crash, `DashBlocked`) plays `BossFight.StunnedState` "Stunned" if a controller has it, else the boss `RoarClip` (Roar), else Stand; `Keep` (phase 2 roar) leaves the Roar on. The switch happens in the same tick as the dash end, before `Charged` fires.
  - Builders: `EnemyRosterBuilder.BuildController` + `CombatBuilder.BuildController` add a "Stand" state = the walk clip at speed 0 (still pose, first frame) to every enemy controller (15 `AC_*.controller` rewritten once). Only bosses play it.
  - Tests: EditMode `EnemyRosterBuilderTests.Controller_HasTheStillStandPose` (13 controllers: 12 roster + zombie / Warden; speed 0, walk motion, bosses have Roar). PlayMode `BossPatternTests.GameScene_Butcher_RecoversStanding_NotOnItsMoveClip`: built Game scene, Chamber 5 Butcher, forces each of its 5 attacks; at Recover the attack state is Stand (Roar after a crash), the animator goes there next frame, at the `Charged` event the state is not Charge.
  - Verified: compile 0 errors / 0 new warnings; scene rebuild changes only the 15 controllers; EditMode 125 pass; PlayMode 628 pass / 2 skipped / 0 fail; mac dev build, `-ss-chamber 5 -ss-boss 0.4 -ss-boss-attack charge -ss-delay 6.3 / 6.9` no exceptions: `Docs/screenshots/kan162-charge-dash.png` (run pose mid-dash), `kan162-charge-stop-stand.png` (after the stop, no run); iOS dev export + xcodebuild + install + launch on iPhone 14 Pro Max OK.
  - Limits: Stand is a frozen walk frame, not a real idle; no skid-stop, recoil or dazed clip yet (follow-up KAN-165; KAN-80, KAN-117). The Charge clip still plays during the wind-up (KAN-80 wind-up pose).

Shader `Art/Shaders/XpOrb.shader` (`M_XpOrb.mat`, one pass, opaque): wrapped main-light shading, emissive core, Blinn hotspot, fresnel rim, a shine band that sweeps over the orb every 1.8 s (vertex colour A = per-orb phase, so orbs do not flash in sync), and a thin dark silhouette edge for white snow (no outline pass). New "Wisp Halos" system: additive soft dot behind each orb (shares `M_FX_SoulMote.mat` with the motes). Resting orbs flash a small white sparkle every 1-2 s (mote system, inside the mote budget).

- 2026-10-15: Pending souls survive an app kill mid-run (KAN-101, branch `feat/kan-101-pending-souls`, from main 93c9b7e). Scheduled-task run run-20261015-1909.
  - Before: run souls went into `SoulWallet` only at a run end (death, Realm cleared, leave to menu). iOS kills background apps, so a kill mid-run lost them.
  - `Core/SoulWallet`: pending amount `wallet.souls.pending` + `.check` (own salt 0x5EED50F2, so a balance pair copied over fails). `SavePending(n)` (n <= 0 deletes), `PendingSouls` (bad checksum = dropped + warning), `ClearPending()`, `RestorePending()` (deletes first, then `Earn`: a crash between the two loses the amount, never doubles it). `Clear()` deletes the pending amount too.
  - `Gameplay/Chamber/RunCounters`: `OnApplicationPause(true)` and `OnApplicationFocus(false)` call `SavePendingSouls()` (saves `Souls` when `RunState.SoulsBanked` is false; a newer save replaces the old one; resume keeps it, so a later crash still keeps the souls). `BankToWallet` deletes the pending amount before it earns: no double count.
  - `UI/MainMenu/MainMenuController.OnEnable`: `SoulWallet.RestorePending()` before the Souls pill reads the balance; a restore shows the toast `RecoveredText(n)` = "N souls recovered from your unfinished journey." for 4 s (as the welcome toast; the 2 s default hid it while the menu faded in). The menu is the only place with no run going (every path from the Game scene to the menu banks first).
  - DevScreenshot `-ss-pending-souls <n>` (after `-ss-wallet`). Screenshot `Docs/screenshots/kan101-menu-souls-recovered.png` (`-ss-wallet 1000 -ss-pending-souls 240 -ss-delay 2.5`: pill 1,240 + toast).
  - Tests: `Tests/EditMode/SoulWalletPendingTests` (6): fresh none, save / replace / zero + negative delete, restore once + Changed, hand-edited pending dropped, balance-salt pair fails, Clear deletes. `Tests/PlayMode/PendingSoulsTests` (5): pause + focus loss save and replace, run end banks once + deletes, banked run saves nothing, kill then restore, menu start on the real UXML banks once with the toast (second start adds nothing). `WalletPrefsGuard` keeps + deletes the pending keys for the suite; `SoulWalletTests` keeps them.
  - Verified: compile 0 errors / 0 new warnings, Game scene rebuild exit 0 (git clean), EditMode 118 pass, PlayMode 632 pass / 2 skipped / 0 fail, mac dev build: menu run restores 240 once (log `[SoulWallet] Restored 240`), second menu run restores nothing, Game run `-ss-horde 40` 115 draw calls, 0 exceptions in all runs; same gate again after merging main (Shadow Ranger art) and the 4 s toast fix; iOS dev export + xcodebuild + install + launch on iPhone 14 Pro Max OK.
  - 2026-10-15 run-20261015-2101: run-1909 never pushed or merged the branch. Merged origin/main (147c267: KAN-34 gold orbs, KAN-166, KAN-169) and re-ran the full gate: scene rebuild exit 0, EditMode 137 pass, PlayMode 635 pass / 2 skipped / 0 fail, mac dev build OK; menu `-ss-wallet 1000 -ss-pending-souls 240`: `Restored 240` once + toast + pill 1,240; second start restores nothing; Game `-ss-horde 40` 115 draw calls; 0 exceptions. Merged to main.
  - Limits: Android also sends `OnApplicationPause`, not tested on a device. A pending amount from an older run is banked at the next menu start even if the player starts a new run right away (wanted). The Mac dev build has no background kill to test by hand; the iPhone check is a kill from the app switcher mid-run, then open the app.

- 2026-10-15: Level-up skill re-roll (KAN-67, branch `feat/kan-67-skill-reroll`, from main 00a18ef). Scheduled-task run run-20261015-2209.
  - Rule: 2 re-rolls per run (not per level-up), `SkillProgression.rerollsPerRun` in `Data/Skills/SkillProgression_Run.asset`. Unused ones carry to the next level-up and the next Chamber; a new run resets them. The chest upgrade screen (KAN-73) has no re-roll.
  - `Gameplay/Chamber/RunState`: `RerollsUsed`, `BonusRerolls`, `RerollsLeft(perRun)`, `AddRerolls(n)` (future relic / reward / skill; debug), `TryUseReroll(perRun)`; `NewRun` resets both.
  - `Gameplay/Skills/SkillManager`: `Roll(count, level, results, exclude)`: the shown cards are left out while more than `count` others remain (else as many as possible, in order); the level's guaranteed skill is not forced again; weights left in each tier are scaled back to the tier's total before the drop (`ExcludeShown`), so tier odds stay those of a normal roll; a re-roll counts as one offer for pity. `OfferableCount(level)`, `RerollsLeft`. `SimulateOffers(..., reroll: true)` = odds of re-rolled offers.
  - `UI/Hud/SkillChoiceScreen`: button `skill-reroll` under the cards (die + "RE-ROLL" + `xN`, GameHud.uxml / .uss `skills__reroll*`, 104 px tall). Tap after `armDelay` (0.4 s), one per 0.3 s, never during a flip: spends one, rolls the new offer, old cards shut along X (0.15 s), new ones open (0.2 s, stagger 0.05 s) and reveal their tier again; cards not pickable during the flip. Disabled at `x0`; "NO OTHER BOONS" + disabled when no other skill could be offered. Key R on desktop. Event `Rerolled(left)`; light haptic.
  - Sound: `sfx_card_reroll_01` (SfxBuilder `CardReroll`: whoosh, 3 card flicks, die knock), `GameAudio.rerolls`, wired by GameSceneBuilder.
  - Dev: menu "Survivor Souls/Debug/Add Re-roll" (play mode; an open screen shows the new count at once); "Print Skill Offer Odds" also prints re-rolled odds. DevScreenshot `-ss-reroll <n>` (with `-ss-xp`).

  - Shots (540x1170, `-ss-scene Game -ss-quality Mobile -ss-run-seed 9001 -ss-immortal -ss-delay 4 -ss-xp 2000 -ss-reroll <n>`): `Docs/screenshots/kan67-reroll-x2.png` (n 0), `kan67-reroll-after-1.png` (n 1), `kan67-reroll-x0-disabled.png` (n 2: dimmed `x0`). Mac Mobile 32 draw calls, 17.0-17.2 ms (vsync).

- 2026-10-15: Versioning, first slice (KAN-172 = KAN-142.1, parent KAN-142, branch `feat/kan-142-versioning`, from main 44df7e7). Scheduled-task run run-20261015-2309. First version **0.2.0 (build 1)**, tag `v0.2.0+1`.
  - `VERSION.json` (repo root) = the one version source: `{ "version": "0.2.0", "build": 1 }`. Only `Tools/bump-version.sh` writes it. Never hand-edit the version in ProjectSettings.
  - `Tools/bump-version.sh patch|minor|major ["KAN-N Title" ...]`: PATCH = fix / polish / follow-up / tooling, MINOR = new feature, MAJOR = store release (user decides). Build +1 on every bump, never reset. Prepends the `CHANGELOG.md` entry (`## X.Y.Z (build N) - date`, one `- KAN-N Title` line per ticket) and prints the tag (`vX.Y.Z+N`). `--check [ref]` fails when the build is not higher than on `origin/main` (merge race); `--show` prints the current version. Env `SS_VERSION_ROOT` / `SS_VERSION_DATE` for tests. Needs bash + python3.
  - `Editor/Platform/VersionStamp` (`IPreprocessBuildWithReport` + `IPostprocessBuildWithReport`, order -100): before every build writes `PlayerSettings.bundleVersion`, iOS + macOS build number, Android `bundleVersionCode`, and `Assets/_Project/Resources/BuildInfo.json` (version, build, short git commit, UTC date). After the build it restores the old values, ProjectSettings.asset and the placeholder byte for byte and clears the dirty flag, so a build leaves no git diff. `DevBuildTools` builds call `VersionStamp.Restore()` in a finally (a failed build gets no postprocess).
  - Runtime `Core/BuildInfo` (`Current`, `DisplayLabel`, `Label(withCommit)`, `Parse`): committed `BuildInfo.json` is the placeholder `0.0.0-dev` (editor shows `v0.0.0-dev`). Main menu bottom (`lbl-version`) and Settings (`lbl-settings-version`, above BACK) show `v0.2.0 (1)`; development builds add the commit (`v0.2.0 (1) 44df7e7`). `TelemetryEvent.Version` / `.Build` filled from `BuildInfo.Current`.
  - Shots (Mac dev, 540x1170): `Docs/screenshots/kan-142-menu-version.png`, `kan-142-settings-version.png` (both `v0.2.0 (1) 44df7e7`). Built `Info.plist`: CFBundleShortVersionString 0.2.0, CFBundleVersion 1.
  - Tests: `Tests/EditMode/VersionStampTests` (8): VERSION.json valid, committed BuildInfo is the placeholder, stamp writes + restore byte for byte (not dirty), restore twice, labels, bad json, telemetry version, bump script (patch / minor / major, build +1, changelog order + format, bad kind writes nothing). `Tests/PlayMode/VersionLabelMenuTests` (menu + Settings label).

- 2026-10-16 (KAN-158): Simple Lit builder materials are validation-stable; specular look decided per family. Branch `feat/kan-158-simplelit-specular`. Scheduled-task run r202610152133-b9aa53.
  - Bug: `EditorAssetUtil.SimpleLitMaterial` wrote `_SpecularHighlights 1` (URP `SpecularSource.NoSpecular`) and forced `_SPECULAR_COLOR` on. URP validation (inspector / test session) removed the keyword, so the shipped look depended on who opened a material and `.mat` files flipped in git. Committed state was mixed: M_Floor / M_Wall / M_Palette (lost `_SPECGLOSSMAP`) without highlight, M_OuterGround / zombies / heart with it.
  - Fix: `SimpleLitMaterial(..., bool specular = true)` sets `_SpecularHighlights` 0 (on) / 1 (off) and runs `ValidateSimpleLit` (= `BaseShaderGUI.SetMaterialKeywords(mat, SimpleLitGUI.SetMaterialKeywords)`). New `EditorAssetUtil.ValidateLikeInspector(mat)` (Simple Lit, Unlit, Particles/Unlit) runs at the end of `TransparentMaterial` + `ParticleMaterial`: a NEW material is written validated (KAN-174 `M_FX_BlackFireSpark.mat` was committed unvalidated and flipped on the next rebuild). Roster builder, heart builder and the menu `LitMaterial` call the same helper after their last change.
  - Look decision: stone floor / wall + palette props (spec atlas) + healing heart = specular ON (the authored spec colours / atlas now really render; subtle under the overcast light). Open ground (`M_OuterGround`, spec 0.04 = invisible) + zombies (`M_Zombie*`) + roster characters = OFF (no plastic sheen on flesh, cheaper pixel on the most drawn materials).
  - Heart: `globalIlluminationFlags` None -> `RealtimeEmissive`. URP validation drops `_EMISSION` without an emissive GI flag, so an opened heart material lost its glow.
  - Rune decal (`M_RuneDecal`, Simple Lit transparent): `TransparentMaterial` sets `_BlendModePreserveSpecular 0` for Simple Lit = straight alpha. The validated premultiplied blend added fog where the decal is clear: the boss arena rune quad showed as a pale square (`Docs/screenshots/kan158-arena-decal-premultiplied-bug.png`). Old file had premultiply keyword + SrcAlpha (double multiply); now plain alpha, looks as before.
  - Tests `Tests/EditMode/MaterialValidationTests` (15): EVERY Simple Lit / Unlit / Particles-Unlit material under `Assets/_Project` equals its URP-validated copy (keywords, queue, GI flags, passes, all floats + colours; names the file and the first difference), SimpleLitMaterial on / off / spec map, transparent + particle builders, shipped look per family, heart keeps `_EMISSION`. A builder that writes an unvalidated material now fails this test.
  - Shots (Mac dev 540x1170 Mobile, `-ss-scene Game -ss-run-seed 9201 -ss-immortal`): `Docs/screenshots/kan158-arena-before.png` / `-after.png` (`-ss-chamber 5 -ss-hearts 2 -ss-player-hp 0.45 -ss-delay 4.5`, crop), `kan158-horde-before.png` / `-after.png` (`-ss-horde 40 -ss-hold 40 -ss-delay 5`, crop). - 2026-10-16: Plague Bloater arms no longer clip in the Burst swell (KAN-146, branch `feat/kan-146-bloater-arm-clip`, run r202610152133-df41d5).
  - Cause: Meshy auto-rig weights the belly and the apron skirt to the thigh bones, so they swing forward with the lunge of the Burst action (386) while the hands hang close: from ~0.75 s the hands slide under the apron skirt, and the inner upper arm sinks into the belly side.
  - Fix (Option A, in Blender): `Tools/Meshy/fix_bloater_burst.py` (header has the command) loads `anim_Burst.glb` of the Meshy job `20261015_031720_enemy-plague-bloater_01a13cb4`, turns both upper arms 15 deg outward round the body's forward axis (eased in over 6 frames = 0.25 s, then held), keys every frame and writes `Art/Characters/Enemies/PlagueBloater/A_PlagueBloater_Burst.fbx` with the same FBX settings as `to_unity.py` (same 68-frame take, meta unchanged). It also prints a per-frame report: arm vertices inside the body by generalised winding number with the Mobile 2-bone skin (the Meshy mesh is a triangle soup, so ray / normal tests lie): worst 12 -> 7 vertices (inner upper arm on the belly side; the armpit seam is ignored). The apron is an open skirt, so the close-ups are the check for it.
  - Rejected: per-frame automatic swing (smallest angle that clears the measure) needed up to 30 deg and bent the clip; runtime bone offsets (Option B) would add a LateUpdate per Bloater.

- 2026-10-16 (KAN-176): Gate arrow sticks to the real screen edge (bug: KAN-63 arrow floated a quarter into the screen). Branch `feat/kan-176-gate-arrow-edge`. Scheduled-task run r202610152214-28ddea.
  - Cause: `GateArrowHud.AllowedRect` removed 400 (top) + 560 (bottom) panel px + 75 (half arrow) + 22 from the safe area; on a 2340-unit tall phone panel the arrow centre stayed 575 / 740 units from the top / bottom edge.
  - Fix (`UI/Hud/GateArrowHud`): edge rect = safe area shrunk by `edgeMillimetres` (2.5 mm, `EdgeMarker.MillimetresToPanel(mm, Screen.dpi or 460 fallback, panel/px)`) + half the arrow, on all 4 sides. Arrow `size` 150 -> 110. `topReserve` / `bottomReserve` / `edgeInset` removed. Distance label removed (UXML `gate-arrow-dist`, USS `.gate-arrow__dist`, `MetresText`, `showDistance`).
  - No-go zones: padded (`zoneGap` 12) `worldBound` of `xp`, `timer`, `boss`, `btn-pause`, `player-hp`, `run-souls`, `lbl-test-mode` + each owned skill icon (children of `skill-row`, not its full-width box) + `JoystickView.IdleBounds` (new: idle ring + chevrons, panel units). HUD rects are re-read only after a `GeometryChangedEvent` (elements + root) or a new skill icon; hidden elements (display none) are skipped. No allocation per frame (test).
  - `EdgeMarker.AvoidZones(point, rect, half, zones, count)` (pure): a point in a zone slides along the SAME edge to the nearest free spot (corner points try both edges). Only when that whole edge line is blocked (full-width XP + health rows at the top) does it step inward just past the blocking zone and slide there (lands between the XP bar and the health row, beside the timer, for a gate straight up). `EdgeMarker.Free` for tests.
  - Dev: `DevScreenshot -ss-exit-offset x,z` puts the Knight at the exit (or arena portal) + offset (0,-40 = gate straight up the screen, 40,0 = gate to the left).

- 2026-10-16 (KAN-159): Skill pick slot-machine roll on each card when the pick opens and after a re-roll. Branch `feat/kan-159-skill-slot-roll`. Scheduled-task run r202610152233-c3a4fe.
  - Flow: the offer is rolled first (`SkillManager.Roll` / `RollUpgrades`, unchanged). Then each card's icon + name scroll top to bottom through the skills that could be offered now (`SkillManager.CollectOfferable(level)`: same rule as `OfferableCount`, no maxed / locked skill; chest screen: `CollectUpgradable` = owned skills below max). Cards stop left to right at 0.6 / 0.8 / 1.0 s on the rolled skill: small bounce of icon + card, clunk, light haptic, then the existing tier reveal (rim colour, tier name, gems, text, pips, badge, Legendary shine / burst). While a card rolls, the rim stays neutral and tier / gems / text / pips / badge are hidden (`visibility`, layout kept). Re-roll (KAN-67): `SwapOffer` starts the roll again on the new cards.
  - Skip + arm: a tap anywhere on the pick (pointer down, trickle-down) or a pick key during the roll lands all reels at once and picks nothing. Picks and the re-roll arm after the last stop + `armDelay` (0.4 s): 1.4 s from open without a skip. No Epic / Legendary slow-down (it would push the total over 1.1 s; dropped as the ticket update allows).
  - Code: `UI/Hud/SkillReel` (pure math: `StopItem`, `Position` = constant speed then linear slow-down to the exact stop item, `Speed01`, `PoolIndex`, `Bounce`). `SkillChoiceScreen`: `reel` switch + data (`reelStops` 0.6/0.8/1.0, `reelSpeed` 16 items/s, `reelDecel` 0.3 s, `reelBounce`, `reelStretch`, `reelTickGap` 0.05 s), `StartRoll` / `UpdateReels` / `Land` / `SkipRoll`, events `ReelTicked` + `ReelStopped`, `IsRolling`, `ReelPool`, `ShownSkill(i)`, `IsStopped(i)`. Per card a 2nd icon `skill-card__reel` (absolute in the plate; the plate clips while rolling) and a one-line reel name `skill-card__reel-name` (absolute sibling of the name, follows the icon nearer the centre; a Label with a child loses its text height). USS block `skill-card--rolling` in `GameHud.uss`. Upper-case names cached once per skill: no allocation per frame (test).
  - `Setup(manager, arm, styles, slotRoll = false)`: code-built screens (older tests) stay without the roll; the scene's screen (AddComponent in `GameSceneBuilder`) has it on. `ChestUpgradeTests` Game-scene test now ticks 1.5 s before its pick.
  - Sound: `SfxBuilder` `sfx_reel_tick_01/02` (40 ms wooden ratchet click) + `sfx_reel_stop_01` (wooden clunk + iron latch, under the tier reveal). `GameAudio`: ticks on one shared voice (a new tick cuts the last), rate limited by the screen; the stop clunk is a hair lower per card.
  - Setting: `GameSettings.FastSkillReveal` (default off), menu Settings toggle "Fast Skill Reveal" (`tgl-fast-reveal`): cards land as before. There is no "Reduce screen effects" setting yet.
  - Dev: DevScreenshot `-ss-xp-lead <s>` (time between the -ss-xp grant and the capture; the pick opens ~0.5 s after the grant); `-ss-reroll` waits for the roll.
  - Shots (Mac dev 590x1280, `-ss-scene Game -ss-xp 40 -ss-immortal -ss-delay 4`): `Docs/screenshots/kan-159-reel-rolling.png` (`-ss-xp-lead 1.15`: all 3 rolling, stretched icons), `kan-159-reel-landing.png` (1.3: card 1 landed, 2-3 rolling), `kan-159-reel-landed.png` (2.6), `kan-159-settings-fast-reveal.png` (`-ss-settings`).
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/SkillReelTests` (10): reel math (0 to the stop item, never backwards, slows down, speed share, pool order, no twin before the stop, bounce); 6 rounds: stops in order at 0.6 / 0.8 / 1.0 s on the rolled offer + reveal on the stop + offer unchanged + reel shows only pool skills + tick rate limit; pool has only offerable skills / chest reel only owned upgradable; skip + arm timing (no skip: 1.4 s; skip: +0.4 s); pointer tap skips; re-roll path rolls again + re-roll waits for the stop; Fast Skill Reveal off; no GC allocation per frame while rolling; shipped scene has the roll + tick clips.

  - Tool: `Editor/Skills/BalanceReport` (menu "Survivor Souls/Debug/Print Balance Report"; batch `-executeMethod SurvivorSouls.Editor.Skills.BalanceReport.PrintFromCommandLine`): power curve + growth at L5/10/15/20 + horde scaling table.

- 2026-10-16 (KAN-93): Hallowed Shield skill (ticket "Temporary Shield"): a ward that blocks all damage for a few seconds on a cooldown. Branch `feat/kan-93-temporary-shield`. Scheduled-task run r202610160208-b2cf31.
  - Skill `temporary_shield` (`Data/Skills/Skill_TemporaryShield.asset`, Epic, 3 levels, level 6 tier, weight 0.6, repeat offers 60 %): ward 3 s every 20 s, 4 s / 17 s, 5 s / 14 s. New stats appended: `StatId.WardDuration` (flat s), `WardCooldown` (flat s, levels -3 / -6 on the effect base 20 s). Cooldown counts from the ward's end; first ward 1 s after the pick (and 1 s after a Chamber loads). Scaled time: pause / level-up freeze both clocks.
  - `Scripts/Gameplay/Skills/Effects/ShieldWardEffect.cs` (prefab `Prefabs/Skills/FX_Skill_HallowedShield`, built by `SkillsBuilder`): sets `PlayerHealth.Warded`, which makes `TakeDamage` return 0 before the shield pool (KAN-82 shield untouched) and raises `OnWardBlocked(amount)`. `Warded` is separate from `Invulnerable` (boss intro), so neither clears the other. Dead Knight = ward dropped, no new ward. Static events `WardRaised / WardBlocked (max 1 per 0.2 s) / WardEnded` for sound; light haptic on a raise.

Pop in (ease-out-back 0.18 s), breathe, gold flash on a blocked hit, flicker 6 -> 14 Hz in the last 0.9 s, swell + fade 0.25 s. Blast factor = blast share x `BlastFoes` / `HitsToKill` (kills per second = crowd DPS / (hits to kill x bolt damage)). - 2026-10-16 (KAN-66): Soul Burst visual rework: a small dark-magic explosion instead of a plain ring. Branch `feat/kan-66-soul-burst-vfx`. Scheduled-task run r202610160509-4b5dc9. Damage logic unchanged.
  - Look (`Gameplay/Skills/Effects/KillBurstEffect.cs`, prefab `FX_Skill_SoulBurst` built by `SkillsBuilder` + new `Editor/Skills/SoulBurstArt.cs`): six pooled Emit-only systems: white-violet core flash with a scale punch (0.1 s), violet ground shockwave (alpha blend: darker than snow, brighter than stone) that ends exactly on the damage radius (`RingSize = reach / 0.4`, follows `BurstRadius` levels), faint additive dome shell, dark soul-fire wisps with a magenta rim + 3 dark smoke puffs (one alpha-blended system, new 128 px `Art/Skills/T_FX_SoulWisp.png`), soul shards that arc out and fall, and a rune mark on the ground (reuses `T_RuneCircle`). New materials `M_FX_SoulBurstGround`, `M_FX_SoulWisp`, `M_FX_SoulBurstMark`. Colours: black / deep purple / magenta / white flash (not red, not gold).

Chain depth is tracked per queued death (Knight kill = 0, burst kill = parent + 1): `ChainScale` fades counts and alpha (fire / smoke by scale squared) so a chain of 20 stays readable. Depth >= 4: one small camera shake (0.07 m, `TopDownFollowCamera.Shake`) + one `Haptics.Light` (rate limited); no shake for single bursts. No GC per burst.

- 2026-10-16 (KAN-199 = KAN-134.1): Ember Bolts burn you can see and feel: ignite flinch, burn slow, flames on burning foes, ignite flare, burning death puff. Branch `feat/kan-134-ember-burn-readable`. Scheduled-task run r202610160508-beafa2. KAN-134 split: .2 blazing bolts = KAN-200, .3 sounds = KAN-201 (blocked by this).
  - Control (`Gameplay/Combat/Enemy.cs`): `ApplySlow(share, s)` (highest share and longest time win, no stacking, cap `MaxSlow` 0.8, bosses take `BossSlowShare` 0.5) lowers `MoveSpeed`; `Stun(s, cooldown)` stops the chase (no walk / turn; crowd push + knockback still move it) and plays the Hit flinch when the controller has one, refused for bosses and while its cooldown runs. Timers in `TickStatus`. Reset on spawn.
  - Skill (`Skills/Effects/BurnOnHitEffect.cs`): every bolt hit also slows the foe by `StatId.BurnSlow` for the burn length (spread fires too). The hit that lights a foe that was not burning = ignite: `Enemy.Stun(StatId.IgniteStun, igniteCooldown 1.5 s)`, flare, static event `BurnOnHitEffect.Ignited` (for KAN-201 sounds). Counters `IgniteCount`, `StunCount`, `BurningDeathCount`.

Card text: "Bolts set foes ablaze, slow 15%", "Fire 40/s for 3 s, slows 18%", "Fire 55/s 4 s, spreads, slow 21%", "Fire 75/s 5 s, 60% blast, slow 25%".

"Flames" (800 max, stretched, 24 tongues/s per burning foe on the body edge, size x body scale x heat; heat = burn dps 25..75) and "Ignite Flare" (flash + 8 puffs + 10 sparks). Material `Art/Skills/M_FX_BurnFlame.mat` is ALPHA BLEND: additive fire was invisible on the Realm 1 snow. Burning death: 6 big flame puffs + 16 embers. The old ember glow tint on the body stays.

Before: `game-kan77-ember-l4-horde60.png`.

Frame time with a full horde on fire not measured on a phone. Confluence balance sheet not updated (KAN-186).

- 2026-10-16 (KAN-136): Attack warnings look medieval: every circle warning is a rune circle burnt into the ground. Branch `feat/kan-136-rune-telegraphs`. Scheduled-task run r202610160543-a101ab.
  - Shader `Art/Shaders/TelegraphCircle.shader` (`SurvivorSouls/TelegraphCircle`, premultiplied alpha blend, no lights, same 2 quads = 2 draw calls per circle as before). Fill (`_Part 0`): dark scorched earth with ground cracks that glow more as the wind-up fills and a bright leading edge on the growing rim; the crack pattern is fixed on the ground (`_FillScale` = fill share), so the fill uncovers it. Ring (`_Part 1`): dark backing (reads on snow), bold outer rim (outer edge ~0.98 = the hit radius), thin inner rim with sundial notches, and a band of 14 abstract occult glyphs that turns 0.3 rev/s while it warns and freezes on the hit. Wave mode (`_Wave 1`, `GroundTelegraph.ShowWave`: Brute slam, boss shockwave): shattered-earth band with glowing cracks at `RimShare` 0.92. Hit (`_Flare`): cracks and glyphs blaze hot, then fade with the flash. Colours come from the tint hue (scorch, ember = hue + warm shift, hot = ember to white), so red (boss / physical), green (Bloater) and violet (Shaman) keep their colour with no code change.
  - Masks: `Editor/Boss/TelegraphArt.cs` paints `T_TelegraphRing.png` (R rims + notches, G glyphs, B wave band, A backing) and `T_TelegraphDisc.png` (R cracks: Voronoi cells + 7 cracks from the centre, G blotches), 256 px, linear, and sets the shader on `M_FX_TelegraphRing.mat` / `M_FX_TelegraphDisc.mat` (same paths and GUIDs: scene references kept). `BossBuilder.CreateTelegraph` calls it (old RingPixel / DiscPixel removed). Tunables on the materials: `_Opacity` 1.35, `_Glow` 0.45, `_Hot` 0.6, `_Wash` 0.12, `_EmberShift`.
  - Runtime: `GroundTelegraph` feeds `_Progress`, `_FillScale`, `_RuneTurns` (Time-based, `runeTurnsPerSecond` 0.3; `Tick` keeps the band turning when an owner stops calling SetProgress), `_Flare`, `_Wave` through its one MaterialPropertyBlock; new read-only `Flare`, `RuneTurns`. `LineTelegraph.Tint(edge, fill, flash)` (same API as the circle; Hide goes back to red). Lanes keep the KAN-167 TelegraphLane look (burnt scorch, cracks, ember chevrons, far-end sigil); that shader only tints the scorch 25 %, so a green / violet lane keeps orange embers (no tinted lane exists yet).
  - Tests: EditMode `TelegraphArtTests` (6: rim bold + backed and nothing past the radius, glyphs only in their band and every glyph has ink, wave band on the rim share, fill has cracks + plain ground, both shipped materials use the shader + mask). PlayMode `TelegraphRuneLookTests` (6: progress + fill share + radius = hit radius, glyph band turns then freezes on the hit + flare, wave mode set / reset, circle tint until Hide, lane tint until Hide, no GC per frame over warn + flash + wave).
  - Shots (Mac dev 540x1170, `-ss-scene Game -ss-run-seed 9001 -ss-immortal -ss-quality Mobile`): `Docs/screenshots/kan136-brute-warn.png` (`-ss-enemy GraveBrute,1 -ss-delay 2.4`), `kan136-brute-wave.png` (3.1: flash + wave), `kan136-brute-flash.png` (3.25: glyphs flare), `kan136-bloater-swell.png` (`-ss-enemy PlagueBloater,1 -ss-delay 2.7`, green), `kan136-ghoul-lane.png` (`-ss-enemy FrenziedGhoul,1,11 -ss-delay 2.0`), `kan136-butcher-chop.png` / `kan136-butcher-spin.png` (`-ss-chamber 5 -ss-boss 0.8 -ss-boss-attack 0|1 -ss-delay 6.4`); seed 9201: `kan136-archer-lane.png` (`-ss-enemy BoneArcher,2 -ss-delay 4.6`), `kan136-shaman-orbs.png` (`-ss-enemy HexboundShaman,1,7 -ss-shaman-cast orbs,1.0 -ss-delay 1.5`, violet). Before = `game-brute-warn.png`, `game-bloater-swell.png`, `game-ghoul-lane.png`, `game-kan105-archer-aim.png`, `game-kan107-shaman-orb-warn.png`, `game-boss-butcher-chop-warn.png`, `game-boss-butcher-spin-warn.png`. Brute warn frame: 33 draw calls (Mobile).
  - Limits / follow-up: on the dark boss arena the red circle reads as a glowing lava sigil (strong; tune `_Glow` down if it is too loud on the phone). Not checked on the iPhone (frame time with 16 circles, glyph readability at phone size). Lane chevrons are not arrow-head runes yet (KAN-167 sigil kept). The shader computes the tint palette per pixel (cheap ALU, 1-2 texture fetches).
- 2026-10-16 (KAN-201 = KAN-134.3): Ember Bolts sounds. Branch `feat/kan-201-ember-sounds`. Scheduled-task run r202610160609-0090fe. Gameplay unchanged.
  - Code: new `Audio/EmberSounds.cs` on the Audio object (`GameSceneBuilder.CreateEmberSounds`, refs `shooter` + `stats`). `GameAudio` not touched. Three layers, each on its own small voice pool with an own "busy until" clock (KAN-66 rule: no `isPlaying`):
    - Ignite: `sfx_ember_ignite_01/02` (gas whoomp + falling low body + hiss / sizzle tail + spark pops, 0.62 s) on `BurnOnHitEffect.Ignited`. 3 voices, 0.08 s rate limit; hotter burn (dps 25..75 = level 1..4) plays lower and louder.
    - Burning: `sfx_ember_crackle_loop_01/02` (seamless 2.25 s: low fire roar + random wood pops). One quiet loop per burning foe, max 3 (2nd / 3rd quieter, pitch 0.9 / 1.12, own start offset). Burning count scanned every 0.2 s from `Enemy.Active`; loops fade in / out at 0.6 / s and fade out while `Time.timeScale` is 0 (level-up screen).
    - Hot shot: `sfx_ember_hot_shot_01/02` (fire fwoosh, band noise 2.4 kHz to 500 Hz + spark ticks, 0.36 s) on `AutoShooter.Fired` while `StatId.BurnDps` > 0. 2 voices, 0.05 s limit, louder at higher levels. Follow-up (Double Shot) bolts get no extra layer.
    - Volume x `GameSettings.SfxVolume`. No GC per hit, shot or frame. Dev builds log one line at quit: `[EmberSounds] ignites played N, dropped N, hot shots N, peak crackle loops N`.
  - Clips: `SfxBuilder.EmberIgnite / EmberCrackle / EmberHotShot`, `Set.EmberIgnites / EmberCrackles / EmberHotShots`.
  - Tests: new PlayMode `EmberSoundsTests` (6): ignite rate limit + 3-voice cap, Ignited event reaches the sound, hot layer only with Ember owned + on the crossbow shot, heat scale, crackle follows the burning count / capped at 3 / fades on pause, no GC. Fixed `EmberBurnControlTests.StunnedFoe_Stands_ThenWalksOn` (KAN-199): it waited 2 frames after the stun; batch frames are ~0.2 ms, so the foe walked < 1 mm and the test failed every batch run. Now it waits up to 0.25 s game time.
  - Verified: Mac dev build `-ss-skills ember_bolts:4,quick_draw:4,multishot:2 -ss-horde 20 -ss-hold 20 -ss-immortal` logged `ignites played 8, dropped 3, hot shots 8, peak crackle loops 3`, 0 exceptions; no Ember = no log line. Not heard on the iPhone: loudness / mix check is KAN-202.

  - Bolt (`Gameplay/Combat/Bolt.cs`): `ApplyLook(heat)` runs only when a pooled bolt's new shot has a different heat (no GC per shot): blazing = `Blaze Head` child on (scale 0.85-1.3 x heat), trail material swapped to `blazeTrailMaterial`, fire gradient (whiter head when hotter, dark red tail), trail time x1.05-1.3, width x3-4.2; heat 0 puts the cached normal trail back. Blazing bolts in flight are in the static `Bolt.Blazing` list (swap-remove on land / disable). `IsBlazing`, `Heat`, `Direction`.

Both ALPHA BLEND: additive fire vanishes on the Realm 1 snow.

Fill: the flat ember chevrons are now carved arrow-head runes (Tiwaz: two arms + a stem, tip to the far end), one per `_ChevronSpacing` 1.3 m, same flow (`_FlowSpeed` 2.6 m/s from the attacker), a dark groove (reads on snow) and a core a step hotter than the cracks that smoulders on its own clock per rune. ## DECISIONS
- Design language: Archero / Archero 2 (menus, loop, chests, daily/weekly quests, gear merge, talents, energy). Look: Dark Souls, realistic, not dismal. User decision 2026-10-11.
- Naming map: energy=Estus, gold=Souls, gems=Ember Shards, chests=Reliquaries, daily=Oaths, weekly=Pilgrimage, chapter=Realm, room=Chamber. See GAME_PLAN sec 2.
- Art source (KAN-27): local TRELLIS.2 for static props/environment; Meshy paid for rigged characters, enemies, bosses, held weapons. Meshy.ai -> FBX -> Unity URP. Paid Meshy plan for ship assets (free = CC BY, needs credit). Tri budget: hero 5-8k, enemy 1.5-3k, boss 8-12k, prop <1.5k.
- Test Mode (KAN-144): every unlock system reads `TestMode.IsOn` (one flag, `settings.testMode`); never write fake progress for it. Hero roster KAN-100, gear KAN-139, Grand Reliquaries KAN-137 wire themselves to it. 2026-10-15.
- Versioning (KAN-142): `VERSION.json` is the one version source; `Tools/bump-version.sh` is its only writer. Every merge to main bumps (PATCH fix / polish / follow-up / tooling, MINOR new feature, MAJOR user decides), build +1 always, `CHANGELOG.md` entry, git tag `vX.Y.Z+N` on the merge commit, closing Jira comment says "Shipped in X.Y.Z (build N)". The build stamp writes PlayerSettings at build time only. Started at 0.2.0 (build 1). 2026-10-15.
- Gate arrow (KAN-63): on the screen edge, never on the ground under the Knight. Render choice (a): Blender model baked to one sprite (cheapest, 1 UI element), not a screen-space mesh. Dim while the portal is locked, bright when open; hidden in the boss fight until the arena portal opens. 2026-10-16.
- Level-up re-roll (KAN-67): 2 per run, not per level-up; not on the chest upgrade screen; a re-roll keeps the tier odds and counts for pity. 2026-10-15.
- Meta upgrades (soul tree KAN-98, later gear / heroes) are permanent `PlayerStats` sources and stay out of the KAN-92 horde power scaling: the horde scales only with in-run skills. 2026-10-15.
- Boss damage (KAN-163): boss hits are a share of the Knight's max health, cap 0.5, phase 2 x1.15; 3 clean hits of a boss's biggest attack kill a fresh Knight; every damaging attack warns >= 0.6 s; contact damage flat and below a telegraphed hit. Armor (KAN-139) will subtract after the share. 2026-10-16.
- Crossbow hold: two hands, crossbow on Spine2 + arm IK baked in Blender script. Recoil procedural in Unity (no extra clip). 2026-10-12 (KAN-11).
- Sounds: procedural (SfxBuilder). No paid packs (Irem rule). 2026-10-12.
- Grass: geometry blades per tile (opaque, no alpha test: cheap on mobile TBDR). 2026-10-12. Not used in Realm 1 since KAN-25 (GrassBuilder, Grass.shader, GrassPusher kept for later realms).
- Realm 1 = snowy seaside shore, bright low-noise floor so the dark Knight reads (KAN-25, 2026-10-13). Terrain = custom mesh + world-space shader, not Unity Terrain.
- Orientation: portrait only, iOS + Android. User decision 2026-10-12.
- Render pipeline: URP. Reason: mobile perf, good look.
- Git LFS: NOT on. git-lfs not installed. Add later before big binary assets.
- Repo: public, github.com/irfancnk/survivor-souls.

- Menu UI: UI Toolkit, not uGUI. Reason: one UXML/USS per screen, easy for AI to edit, cheap on mobile.
- Scenes built by editor script. Reason: reproducible, diffable.
- Placeholder art = procedural (no Meshy access yet). One palette material for characters/props. Reason: few draw calls, swap later.
- Game lighting: realtime unshadowed point lights + trilight ambient, no bake. Reason: simple, fast on mid phones. Bake later if needed.
- Gameplay camera: Archero style, fixed yaw 0, pitch 55.
- Font: Unity default for now. No font download.
- Tracking: Jira site survivorsouls.atlassian.net, project SurvivorSouls (key KAN). One ticket per user task. In-dev status name = "In Development".
- Docs: Confluence space SS = source of truth for design docs. Repo Docs/ keeps copy. New doc goes to right numbered folder; old doc to 99 Archive (no delete).

## KNOWN ISSUES
- Mac build needs `-buildTarget OSXUniversal` on Unity command line. Else URP SSAO/post shaders stripped, PC pipeline fails.
- Unity build rewrites Assets/Settings/*RPAsset + GraphicsSettings (prefilter fields). Do not commit that noise.
- Since KAN-157 a Game / MainMenu scene rebuild leaves generated controllers, tile prefabs, scenes and roster materials unchanged. If `git status` shows them after a rebuild, the builder output really changed (see `[StableAssetWriter] Changed:` in the log).
- `Mathf.SmoothStep` is an interpolator, not GLSL smoothstep. Use `TextureFactory.SmoothStep`.
- Batch Unity in a worktree killed a lingering IL post-process runner of the open editor once. Harmless, editor respawns it.
- Archer is placeholder quality (primitive look). Replace with Meshy model.
- Agent cannot screenshot Mac screen: `screencapture` has no Screen Recording permission. Use dev build + `-ss-screenshot` instead.

- 2026-10-13: XP system: soul wisps drop from enemies, XP bar, level-up flow (KAN-21, branch `feat/kan-21-xp-wisps`, from KAN-16 head). Scheduled-task run.
  - Data: `Progression/XpCurve` ScriptableObject (explicit table first, then `base * growth^(level-1)` rounded). Asset `Data/Progression/XpCurve_Run.asset`: table 30, 50, 70, then base 50, growth 1.22, round 5. Builder never overwrites an existing asset (designer edits kept). `Enemy.xpValue` (zombie 10, set by `CombatBuilder`).
  - `Progression/XpManager` ("Progression" object): Level, Xp, XpToNext, TotalXp. `AddXp` carries remainder; one big gain = one `OnLevelUp(level)` per level, in order. Run-only: fresh in Awake (retry reloads scene) + `ResetRun()`.
  - `Progression/SoulWispField` ("Souls" object): no GameObject per wisp. Struct array (capacity 128, swap-remove), drawn as billboards of ONE particle system via `SetParticles` (alpha blend, `T_SoulWisp`: white core, shell, darker rim) + ONE additive mote system (trail motes, absorb flash). 2 draw calls for any count, no lights, no allocs. States Pop (jump up, gravity, land at 0.45 m) -> Rest (bob + pulse) -> Drift (after 10 s, to player, max 8 m/s) / Magnet (inside pickup radius: lift, ease-in accel 40 m/s^2 to the chest, absorb at 0.35 m, 1.5 s timeout). Full pool: drop merges into the oldest wisp (XP never lost). Tiers by XP: small < 25 pale blue 0.42 m, elite < 100 cyan 0.62 m, boss gold 0.95 m. Pickup haptic via `Haptics.Light` with own 0.12 s limit. Event `Collected(point, xp)`.
  - `Player/SoulCollector` on player: `PickupRadius` = base 2.6 m x `RadiusMultiplier` (hook for KAN-19 skills). In PlayerDeath disable list (dead Knight collects nothing).
  - `Progression/LevelUpFlow`: queues level-ups; per level: `GamePause.Acquire`, `Started` (FX, sound, pop text), 0.55 s unscaled, then `LevelUpChoice.Present(level, onDone)`; pause held across queued levels, released after the last pick. Double callback ignored. Queue dropped on player death.
  - `LevelUpChoice` = abstract MonoBehaviour. KAN-19 skill cards subclass it and get set on `LevelUpFlow.choice`. Placeholder now: `UI/Hud/LevelUpBanner` ("LEVEL N / YOUR SOUL GROWS STRONGER" band in upper third, 1.4 s, no input).
  - `Core/GamePause`: hold count, time scale 0 while held, restores previous scale. `Clear()` on `SceneLoader.LoadAsync` and `GameSceneController.OnDestroy`.
  - FX: `FX/LevelUpFx` on player (ground ring from `T_Ring`, chest flash, rising sparks; unscaled-time particle systems).
  - HUD: `UI/Hud/XpHud`: top row "LV N" + soul-blue bar (above the HP row), eased fill on unscaled time, wraps on level-up, glow flash; "LEVEL UP" pop over the Knight. Styles `hud-xp*`, `levelup*` in GameHud.uss. All picking ignore.
  - Sound: `SfxBuilder` adds `sfx_soul_chime_01..02` (glass bell) + `sfx_level_up_01` (C-E-G-C arpeggio + swell). `GameAudio`: chime pitch +1 semitone per pickup in a streak (0.6 s window, cap 9), 35 ms chime limit; level-up plays during pause.
  - Builder: `Editor/Progression/ProgressionBuilder` (called by GameSceneBuilder).
