- 2026-10-13: Skill system, skills picked level by level (KAN-19, branch `feat/kan-19-skills`, from KAN-21 head). Scheduled-task run.
  - Stats: `Skills/PlayerStats` on the player = one stat sheet. Sources (skills) set `StatModifier`s (Flat or Percent, per stack); `Modify(id, base)` = (base + flat) * (1 + percent), cached per stat, no alloc. Consumers keep their base numbers and read the sheet: `AutoShooter` (Damage, AttackSpeed = shots/s, CritChance, CritDamage, Multishot, Pierce, Ricochet), `PlayerMover.MaxSpeed` (MoveSpeed), `PlayerHealth` (MaxHealth: raise heals the gain, drop clamps), `SoulCollector.PickupRadius`. New `StatId` values go at the END of the enum (assets store numbers).
  - Data: `SkillDefinition` SO (id, name, text, icon, rarity Common/Rare/Epic, maxStack, weight, modifiers, optional effect prefab). `SkillProgression` SO: tiers (fromLevel, unlocks, rarity weights; pool = all tiers at or below the level, odds = highest tier) + guarantees (level -> skill). Assets `Data/Skills/Skill_*.asset` (12) + `SkillProgression_Run.asset`: L2 stat skills + Keen Eye + Piercing (75/25/0), L4 + Ricochet, Ember Bolts, Blood Covenant (60/32/8), L6 + Multishot, Soul Burst (48/34/18). Guarantees: L4 Ember Bolts, L6 Multishot. Builder never overwrites existing skill/table assets (only fills missing icon/effect links).
  - Skills: Sharpened Bolts +20% dmg (x5), Quick Draw +15% atk speed (x5), Fleet Foot +10% move (x3), Vigor +80 HP (x5), Soul Siphon +40% pickup (x3), Keen Eye +10% crit +25% crit dmg (x4), Piercing Bolts +1 (x3), Ricochet +1 bounce 6.5 m (x3), Ember Bolts 25 fire dps 3 s (x3), Blood Covenant 5% life steal (x3), Multishot +1 bolt fan 11 deg / -15% dmg (x2), Soul Burst 50% bolt dmg in 2.6 m on any kill, chains (x3).
  - `SkillManager` ("Progression" object): owned skills + stacks (pick order), `Grant`, `CanTake`, `Roll(count, level, list)` (distinct, maxed out, guarantee first, weighted rarity x skill weight without replacement, zero weight only when nothing else left, no alloc), `ResetRun` (also in Awake: run-only). Seed field (0 = random).
  - Behaviour skills = `SkillEffect` subclass on a prefab (`Prefabs/Skills/FX_Skill_*`), spawned under the player on the first stack, strength read from stats: `BurnOnHitEffect` (Enemy.ApplyBurn, 0.5 s ticks, ember glow tint, embers from 1 shared particle system), `LifeStealEffect` (heal share of `BoltHit.Damage`, crimson motes), `KillBurstEffect` (kills queued in a ring buffer, resolved next Update: no edit of Enemy.Active inside the damage event; ring + sparks).
  - Combat: `Bolt.Launch(pos, dir, BoltShot, release, onHit)`; nearest crossing per step, never hits an enemy twice, pierce then ricochet. `AutoShooter.EnemyHit(BoltHit)` event, `Fire(target)` public, `CurrentDamage`, `CurrentInterval`. `Enemy.TakeDamage(amount, dir, DamageKind Direct/Burn/Burst, crit)`; only Direct knocks back + flashes. `LastDamageKind`/`LastHitCrit` valid during `Enemy.Damaged`. HitMarkerHud + hit tick sound ignore non-direct non-kill hits. CombatHud: crit "N!" gold, burn small orange, burst pale blue.

- 2026-10-13: Off-screen enemy spawning (KAN-29, branch `feat/kan-29-enemy-spawn`, from main after the integration merge). Scheduled-task run.
  - `Combat/EnemySpawner` rewritten. Where: 12 stratified ring candidates; each pushed out until the body (feet y 0 and head y 2.1) is `screenMargin` 2.5 m outside the live camera's view and at least `minDistance` 12 m from the player, + 0-3 m jitter. View = `Combat/ScreenFootprint` (viewport corner rays cut a plane: convex quad; signed distance + ray exit). Live camera, so any aspect, zoom or boss camera works. Then: walkable (`World/IWalkability`), not within 2.5 m of a spawn of the last 1.2 s (groups spread). Weighted pick: ahead / beside the movement 1, straight behind 0.15 (standing: bias decays to 0.35 of the last heading). No valid point = no spawn, retry after 0.3 s.
  - Gates: `Combat/SpawnGate` (static `Active`, spread 1 m, weight 1.5). `WorldBuilder` puts one at the pier foot (`Tile_ShorePier`) and beside the wreck (`Tile_ShoreWreck`); tiles are pooled, so gates come and go with them. Gate must be 12-32 m away and off screen.
  - Walkability: `WorldStreamer : IWalkability`. `IsBlocked(pos, r)` = outside `PlayableMin/Max` or inside a tile box collider (y band 0.05-2 m). Tile prefab + rotation is a pure function of the seed (`ResolveTile`), boxes cached per prefab: loaded and not-yet-loaded tiles answer the same, no physics, no alloc.
  - Waves: `Combat/EnemyWaveTable` SO, asset `Data/Combat/EnemyWaves_Realm1.asset` (builder never overwrites; fills empty prefab slots). Phases by run time: 0 s = baseline (6 alive, 1 per 1.4 s); 45 s = 8 alive, groups 1-2 / 1.6 s, burst 3 every 25 s; 120 s = 10 alive, groups 1-2 / 1.5 s, burst 4 every 22 s. Weighted enemy types per phase. Prewarm 12 per type. KAN-30 owns the horde numbers: edit the asset.
  - Recycle: every 0.5 s, up to 2 hidden enemies farther than 30 m go to a new spawn point (`Enemy.Relocate`, keeps HP and burn). Visible enemies are never moved.
  - Pool per enemy type (prefab -> stack), prewarmed in Start. `Placed(enemy, pos, recycled)` event (tests; spawn FX later). `SpawnAt` public (boss spawners KAN-22/23 can use `TryFindSpawnPoint` + `SpawnAt`).
  - Steering (`Enemy`): static `Enemy.Walkability` (set by the spawner). Probe 0.9 m ahead; blocked -> smallest free turn (35 deg steps, up to 140), side kept while the detour lasts (no dithering); a step into an obstacle slides along the free axis. Body radius 0.35.
  - Audit + debug: every placement checks `Camera.WorldToViewportPoint` of feet and head; inside = `OnScreenSpawns` + warning. Counters (`attempts / candidates / rejectedOnScreen / blocked / crowded / range / noPoint / spawned / recycled / onScreenSpawns`) logged every 60 s in dev builds and by DevScreenshot before capture. Gizmos: feet + head view quads, frustum, 12 m and 30 m rings, last 64 candidates (green valid, red on screen, grey blocked, blue crowded, magenta range), recent spawns.
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/EnemySpawnTests` (17 + 2 explicit soak): off screen + margin + min distance on tall / short / tablet / zoomed camera (400 each), blocked ground never used, heading bias, no point = no spawn + throttled retry, group spread, gate used / on-screen gate rejected, recycle off screen keeps HP, visible enemy never recycled, prewarmed pool + no GC alloc, wave table, steering round a wall, Game scene soak 150 s tall + short. All 93 PlayMode tests pass.
  - 10-minute acceptance soak (`-testCategory Soak`, real Game scene, immortal Knight wandering, time x8): tall 71 placements, short 66, onScreenSpawns 0, no blocked / too-close placement, pool never grew (created 12).
  - Screenshots `Docs/screenshots/game-spawn-shore.png`, `game-spawn-walk-in.png` (zombies entering at the screen edge).
  - Verified: scene rebuild 0 errors, mac dev build OK, no exceptions. Not on a phone this run.
  - Not done: Chamber system (no per-Chamber spawn point lists yet: ring + tile gates instead), boss telegraph (KAN-22/23), navmesh (simple steering only; a U-shaped obstacle can still trap a chaser), AI time slicing (KAN-30).

All slower than the Knight (5). Material per type (tint baked, no property block when calm: SRP Batcher).

  - `AutoShooter` reload is now a 0..1 fraction (`ReloadProgress`), grows by dt / `CurrentInterval` each frame (attack speed stat, never a separate timer). Speed change mid-sweep keeps the fraction, only the rate changes (no arc jump). Volley fires in the frame the fraction hits 1; back-to-back fire carries the frame overshoot (no rate drift at 30 FPS). New: `ReloadCompleted` event, `Engaged` (standing still + target in range), public `Tick(dt)` for tests. Multishot volley = one `Fired` = one sweep (Double Shot KAN-36 will be the same).
  - `Gameplay/Player/ReloadRing` (on player): reads the shooter, one MPB write per frame, no alloc. States: reloading (track + bright arc + head, clockwise from 12 o'clock), ready flash 0.1 s, fire pulse 0.16 s (ring kicks outward), calm full dim ring when moving / no target (no empty sweeps). Ring quad kept world-aligned (12 o'clock = screen top when the Knight turns). Fades to 75% while running. Scaled time: freezes with `GamePause` (level-up, pause) and app background. Dev builds count `EarlyShots` (volley before full sweep) + warning; tests assert 0.
  - Shader `Art/Shaders/ReloadRing.shader` (unlit, premultiplied alpha, atan2 radial fill, AA via fwidth, dark outline for snow, soft glow, no texture). Material `Art/Materials/Game/M_ReloadRing.mat` holds style values (colours, radius, thickness, outline, glow, opacity 0.85); builder does not overwrite them (delete the mat to reset to shader defaults). Quad 1.4 m, ring radius 0.49 m. Old `M_HeroRing.mat` removed (`T_Ring` still used by Direction Marker).
  - Settings: "Reload Ring" toggle in the main menu Settings modal (`GameSettings.ReloadRing`, default on).
  - Tests: `Tests/PlayMode/ReloadRingTests` (13): fire frame == sweep end frame (60 FPS and uneven frame times), 10 shots in 10 s, attack speed +100% mid-sweep (no jump, rest at 2x, next sweep 0.5 s), multishot = one sweep, no target = calm, target appears = fires + new sweep, flash / pulse decay, moving = calm + no shot, pause freezes sweep / flash / pulse, Settings hides ring, world-aligned, shader exists, no GC per frame. - 2026-10-13: Bug fix, zombies stop walking after attacking (KAN-43, branch `feat/kan-43-zombie-walk-restore`, from main d8eb1c7). Scheduled-task run.
  - `Combat/Enemy.UpdateAnimation`: (1) paused frame (`dt <= 0`) returns early: before, speed = 0/0 = NaN poisoned `_groundSpeed` and froze `animator.speed` after every pause / level-up screen (this fix was uncommitted in the main checkout since 16:26, now on main). (2) Hysteresis: attack starts at contact range + 0.15 m, ends only beyond contact range + `AttackRelease` 0.45 m. (3) `MinStateHold` 0.3 s between walk <-> attack switches (crowd jostle cannot keep the animator in half cross-fades). (4) Self-heal every `AnimCheckInterval` 0.25 s (staggered): when the animator rests (no transition) in the state the code does not want, cross-fade again; counter `Enemy.AnimatorResyncs` (debug / tests).   - `SkillManager` (the one place that changes levels): `Owned.Level` (was `Stacks`), `GetLevel(skill|id)` (was `StacksOf`), `FindSkill(id)` (owned, then `SkillProgression.FindSkill`: tier unlocks + guarantees), `CanTake` (new or below max, level-up offers), `CanUpgrade` (owned and below max), `Grant(skill|id)` (level-up pick, new or +1, raises `Granted`), `Upgrade(skill|id, levels = 1)` (chests: owned only, +N capped, no `Granted`), event `SkillChanged(skill, old, new)` on Grant / Upgrade (not on `Restore` / `ResetRun`).   - New stats (end of `StatId`): `BurnDuration` (s, added to `BurnOnHitEffect.duration` 3 s; `BurnOnHitEffect.Duration`), `BurstRadius` (m, added to `KillBurstEffect.radius` 2.6 m; `KillBurstEffect.Radius`, ring FX size follows).
  - Builder: `SkillsBuilder.Specs` holds the tables (`Levels`). New asset = created with the table. Existing asset with an EMPTY table = filled once (`FillLevels`). An asset that has a table is never touched: tune in the Inspector.
  - Card texts max 35 chars (test). Still 3 lines on a 540 px wide phone for some (Blood Covenant L4, Ricochet L4): layout fix = KAN-76.
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/SkillLevelTableTests.cs` (62): full stat sheet for every skill at every level vs the balance sheet (56 cases), sheet covers every skill to max, tier level rules + text per level + row 1 = description/modifiers, primary stat never drops + every level changes something, Sharpened upgrade to max (195 dmg, never offered again), Ember Bolts burn 3/3/4/5 s, Soul Burst radius 2.6/2.6/3.2/3.6 m + reset. Full suite: 269 pass, 2 skipped (soak), 0 fail.

- 2026-10-14: Reliquary Hunter skill + chest drops (KAN-72 = KAN-50.3, branch `feat/kan-72-reliquary-drops`, from main ed32e26). Scheduled-task run.
  - Skill: `Data/Skills/Skill_ReliquaryHunter.asset` (id `reliquary_hunter`, Epic, 5 levels, chest icon `SkillIconPainter.Chest`). Pure data, no effect prefab: levels set new stats (end of `StatId`) `ChestChance` / `ChestEliteChance` (L1 1 % / 8 %, L2 1.5 / 10, L3 2 / 12, L4 2.5 / 14, L5 3 / 16), `ChestChoices` (+1 upgrade card from L3), `RareChestEvery` (5 at L5). Joins the level 4 tier of `SkillProgression_Run.asset` (`SkillsBuilder.LateUnlocks`: added once when the table does not list it; set weight 0 to take it out). Decision: level 4 tier, not 3 (no tier at 3; Epic odds there are 3 %).
  - Offer gate: `SkillDefinition.RequiresUpgradableSkill` + `SkillManager.CanOffer` / `HasUpgradableSkill`: not offered as new until another owned skill can still level up. `Roll` uses `CanOffer`; `Grant` and forced (dev) offers skip the gate.
  - Drops: `Gameplay/Chests/ChestDropper` ("Chests" scene object, `ChestBuilder`). Listens to kills; elite = boss or XP >= 25 (Brute). Rules in `Data/Chests/ChestDropConfig.asset` (made once, never overwritten): pity 90 s of game time (only while the skill is owned) then the next kill always drops, max 2 on the ground (a kill while full drops nothing; pity waits), lifetime 40 s with a 5 s blink that speeds up 2 -> 7 Hz, base 2 cards, Rare = 2 upgrades, scatter 0.8 m on free ground. Events `Dropped` / `Expired` (telemetry hooks for KAN-75). `DropNow`, `Remove` (for KAN-73 pickup), `Simulate(kills, eliteShare, secondsPerKill, seed)` -> `DropStats` (no spawning, own RNG). Chests and pity are per Chamber (lost on the portal, KAN-50 proposal).
  - Chest: `ReliquaryChest` (pooled, ticked by the dropper, scaled time = pauses with the cards): procedural wooden chest (palette mesh, 1 draw), gold beam (crossed quads 1.2 x 4.2 m, alpha blend so it reads on snow; Rare = blue), ground glow, land pop. Prefab `Prefabs/Chests/Chest_Reliquary.prefab`, art in `Art/Chests/`. Holds `Tier`, `Choices`, `Upgrades` for KAN-73.
  - Debug (Play mode): "Survivor Souls/Debug/Chest: Drop Now", "Chest: Print Drop Statistics" (1000 kills, 10 % elites, a kill every 0.6 s). DevScreenshot `-ss-chests <n>` (Light, Rare, ... ahead of the Knight).
  - Shots: `-ss-scene Game -ss-delay 4 -ss-skills sharpened_bolts,reliquary_hunter -ss-chests 2` (540x1170) -> `Docs/screenshots/game-reliquary-chests.png`; `-ss-skills quick_draw -ss-xp 2000 -ss-offer reliquary_hunter,sharpened_bolts,keen_eye` -> `game-reliquary-hunter-card.png`.
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/ChestDropTests` (17 incl. 5 statistics cases): shipped levels, no drops / no pity without the skill, 40 x 1000 simulated kills per level within 10 % of the target rate (0.9 x normal + 0.1 x elite), paced run keeps >= 1 chest / 90 s, pity, cap (pity waits), blink + expiry + pool reuse, zero dt freezes, Rare every 5th with 2 upgrades and 3 cards, real kills (normal vs elite chance, landing near the body, disabled = deaf), offer gate, shipped config / prefab / tier, Game scene (dropper wired, pool prewarmed, grant -> 1 %, chest shows). `SkillLevelTableTests` now 13 skills (Reliquary rows; 5 levels allowed for this utility Epic). - 2026-10-14: Chest pickup + upgrade screen (KAN-73 = KAN-50.4, branch `feat/kan-73-chest-upgrade`, from main 7317433). Scheduled-task run.
  - Pickup: `ChestDropper` gets `player` + `skills`. The living Knight within `ChestDropConfig.PickupRadius` (1.1 m, ground plane) of a chest older than `PickupDelay` (0.3 s) opens it: off the ground (`Opening` list, no expiry, frees its cap slot), event `OpenStarted`, `ReliquaryChest.BeginOpen` / `TickOpen` (lid swings 110 deg on its back hinge, body jumps, beam flares wide, fade) for `OpenTime` 0.4 s, then `Opened` (contents still valid) and back to the pool. Scaled time: freezes under a level-up screen. No drop roll (not even pity) while no owned skill can level up (`SkillManager.HasUpgradableSkill`). New config fields have defaults, the shipped asset keeps them without a rewrite.
  - Prefab: lid is its own part `Body/Lid` (`Mesh_ChestLid.asset`, pivot on the hinge); body got a gold "treasure" floor seen when open.
  - Rewards: `Gameplay/Chests/ChestRewards` (on "Chests"): `Opened` -> `LevelUpFlow.EnqueueUpgrade(UpgradeOffer(choices, levels, rare))`. Fallback when nothing can level up or the queue is full: heal `FallbackHeal` 25 % of max HP + `FallbackXp` 30 XP as 3 soul wisps out of the chest. Light haptic on open. Decision: "extra Souls" of KAN-50 = XP soul wisps (no Souls currency in the run yet).
  - One modal pipeline: `LevelUpFlow` now also queues chest upgrades (`UpgradeOffer` in `LevelUpChoice.cs`, cap `UpgradeQueueCap` 3). Pending level-ups always go first; a chest shows straight away (no celebration), pause held across the queue, waits while another system holds `GamePause` (Chamber fade, victory), dropped on death. Events `UpgradeStarted` / `UpgradeFinished` / `UpgradeEmpty` (the chest's turn found nothing to upgrade -> fallback). `LevelUpChoice.PresentUpgrade` (virtual, false = nothing to show).
  - Cards: `SkillManager.RollUpgrades(count, list)` = owned skills below max only, distinct, weight 1 / (1 + times already on a chest screen this Chamber) so the same skill is not on every chest; one card when only one can level; honours `ForceNextOffer` (dev). `SkillChoiceScreen.PresentUpgrade`: same cards and tier look, band "RELIQUARY" / "RARE RELIQUARY" (USS `skills--chest`, `skills--chest-rare`: gold / soul blue), subtitle "UPGRADE A SKILL" (+N LEVELS on a Rare chest), badge `LV N → N+k` and the text of level N+k (k = chest levels capped at max), pips mark the new levels, pick = `SkillManager.Upgrade(skill, levels)` + event `Upgraded(skill, old, new)`. Subtitle label now named `skill-choice-sub`.
  - Sound: `SfxBuilder` `sfx_chest_drop_01` (wood thud, strap rattle, D6 bell), `sfx_chest_open_01` (creak, lid knock, A-C#-E-A gold arpeggio); `GameAudio` plays them on `Dropped` / `OpenStarted` and the boon strike a fifth up on `Upgraded`.
  - Debug: menu "Survivor Souls/Debug/Chest: Open Upgrade Screen" (Play mode). DevScreenshot `-ss-open-chest light|rare` (chest at the feet, the real pickup opens it; force cards with `-ss-offer`).
  - Shots (540x1170): `-ss-scene Game -ss-delay 4 -ss-skills sharpened_bolts,ember_bolts,ember_bolts,quick_draw -ss-open-chest light` -> `Docs/screenshots/game-chest-upgrade-screen.png`; `-ss-skills ricochet,blood_covenant,blood_covenant,soul_burst -ss-offer ricochet,blood_covenant,soul_burst -ss-open-chest rare` -> `game-chest-upgrade-rare.png`.
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/ChestUpgradeTests` (19): roll (owned below max only, one card, rotation within 80-120 of 400, forced), walk-in (landing delay, radius, height ignored, lid, Opened, pooled), dead Knight, opening = no expiry + free cap slot, no drops with nothing to upgrade, flow (pause, owned cards, +1 exactly, resume), Rare +2 capped, level-up first, pile of 4 = 3 screens in sequence + 1 fallback (heal + 3 wisps), fallback with nothing upgradable, empty turn, other pause holder, death, texts, Reliquary look on the real HUD uxml and back to the level-up look, shipped config + hinged lid, Game scene end to end (walk-in -> screen -> pick). - 2026-10-14: Bug fix, long skill text ran into the level pips on the card (KAN-76, branch `feat/kan-76-card-text-pips`, from main 16c7219). Scheduled-task run.
  - Cause: card had a fixed `height: 560px`. A 2-line name + 3-line text (Blood Covenant L4 "Heal 12% of damage, +60 max HP") filled it, the spacer above the pips collapsed to 0 and the last line sat on the pips (gap 0 px). Same card on the level-up and the Reliquary chest screen.
  - Fix (`UI/Hud/GameHud.uss` only): `.unity-button.skill-card` `min-height: 560px` (was `height`), so a long card grows and the row stretches the other cards of the offer to the same height; `flex-shrink: 0` on `.skills__band`, `.skills__cards` and the card parts (rarity, marks, plate, name, desc, pips), so no label is squashed; `.skill-card__desc` 16 px bottom margin = clear space above the pips. Longest card is ~600 panel px; fits 16:9 (1920) and 19.5:9 (2340) panels.
  - Tests `Tests/PlayMode/SkillCardLayoutTests` (2): real HUD uxml, every shipped skill (13) at every level in groups of 3 (longest names together) + the longest chest texts on a Rare chest: name and text boxes get the measured height of their wrapped lines (4 px slack), >= 12 px between the last line and the pips, pips inside the card, equal card heights. Red on the old USS (blood_covenant L4 gap 0), green on the fix. Full suite: 312 pass, 2 skipped (soak), 0 fail.
  - Shots: `-ss-scene Game -ss-delay 4 -ss-skills ricochet x3,blood_covenant x3,soul_burst x3 -ss-xp 2000 -ss-offer ricochet,blood_covenant,soul_burst` at 540x1170 -> `Docs/screenshots/game-skill-cards-long-text-540.png`, at 1080x1920 -> `game-skill-cards-long-text-1080.png`; `-ss-skills ricochet,blood_covenant,blood_covenant,ember_bolts -ss-offer ricochet,blood_covenant,ember_bolts -ss-open-chest rare` -> `game-skill-cards-chest-long-text.png`.
  - Verified: compile 0 errors, Game scene rebuild exit 0 (only fileID / order churn, not committed), mac dev build + 3 screenshot runs without exceptions, iOS dev export + xcodebuild + install on iPhone 14 Pro Max OK; launch refused, phone locked.
  - Limits: the 35-char card text rule (SkillLevelTableTests) stays; a 4-line text would still fit (card grows) but reads heavy, keep texts short. Card visual rework = KAN-38.
